## CAPRI Definition

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The Compensation and Pension Record Interchange (CAPRI) project is an information technology initiative to improve service to disabled Veterans by promoting efficient communication between the Veterans Health Administration (VHA) and Veterans Benefits Administration (VBA). Online access to medical data enhances the timeliness of the benefits determination. Previous attempts to automate this process were hindered by the "roll and scroll" nature of the VHA computer interface of the Automated Medical Information Exchange (AMIE) II. The CAPRI software acts as a bridge between the VBA and VHA information systems. It offers VBA Rating Veteran Service Representatives and Decision Review Officers help in building the rating decision documentation through online access to medical data. It also offers VHA Compensation and Pension (C&P) staff an easy, standardized way of recording C&P Examination reports.

CAPRI provides VBA employees with a standardized, user-friendly method to rapidly access Veterans' electronic medical records throughout the Department of Veterans Affairs (VA). CAPRI delivers leading edge "point and click" technology to the users' desktops. In addition, the learning curve for CAPRI is significantly less than that for character-based systems. CAPRI builds upon existing VHA information security approaches and uses established mechanisms to ensure only authorized access to medical data. CAPRI adds a level of security by allowing VBA users to read but not alter electronic medical record information. CAPRI also provides innovative improvements for medical centers by integrating highly detailed C&P Rating examination results into the Veterans' medical records. Previously, these reports were not retained online in medical center computer systems but were archived onto paper. This procedure precluded the sharing of clinically useful data.

Initially developed specifically for VBA, the utility of CAPRI has been expanded to other user groups that include VHA, Office of the Medical Inspector, Office of Information (OI), Research, and Veteran Service Officers. Recently, most of the newest features of CAPRI are specifically targeted at adding features to be used by VHA C&P providers and staff.

## CAPRI Application Features

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Demographics

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

- Load new patients into the Veterans Information Systems Technology Architecture (VistA) system.
- View patient demographics
- Report patient address changes to VHA.
- View patient temporary address

### C&P Examination Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

- Add new C&P exam request.
- Edit unreleased C&P exam request.
- Add exams to C&P exam requests.
- Cancel C&P exam requests.
- Create an insufficient exam request.
- Re-route an exam request.
- Track individual and cumulative pending exams.
- Request Veterans Affairs Form (VAF) 7131 information.
- Create VA Regional Office reports.
- Create Automated Management Information System (AMIS) 290 reports.
- Create insufficient exam report.
- Send Automatic Mailman bulletins to AMIE mail groups.

### Patient Records Navigation

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

- View health summaries.
- View appointment lists.
- View discharge summaries.
- View consultation requests and results.
- View cumulative vitals.
- View active medications.
- View lab reports.
- View imaging.
- View procedures.

### Reports

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

- All existing AMIE reports for VBA are available.
- Custom C&P template tracking reports for VHA are available.
- Other C&P activity reports not available in AMIE.
- Generates a report for 8861 Request for Medical Services, Chapter 31.

### C&P Exam Entry

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

- All standard AMIE worksheets are available in template form.
- Automatic sending of completed exam requests.
- Ability to save template work in progress and finish later.
- Ability for site to review exams before releasing it to VBA.
- Multiple templates can be merged into a single exam.
- Ability to add multiple medical opinions to the DBQ Medical Opinion

## Additional Information

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Additional information and downloads are available on the VA Intranet.

- [CAPRI Documentation](http://www.va.gov/vdl/application.asp?appid=133)
- Office of Disability and Medical Assessment (DMA)
- [PIV Binding](https://yourit.va.gov/kb_view.do?sysparm_article=KB0013359)
  - Process for linking enterprise identity/IAM identity \[SecID\], including their VA AD and VA PIV with a VistA account

### Points of Contact

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

User support questions should be addressed to local IT support staff, Information Resources Management (IRM), or one of the National Service Desks. A ServiceNow ticket may be submitted for CAPRI related issues to the National Service Desk.

### Installation

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

> **NOTE:** It is important that all users at your site remain on the same version.

For the VBA, the new version runs when the user starts the application from Start/Vbapps/CAPRI/CAPRI. The user can make new Windows desktop shortcuts after starting the new version.

For the VHA, the IRM department installs the CAPRI desktop icon. Please check with them on the specifics of starting CAPRI. Normally, the user should find the CAPRI shortcut in the same place the user would find the Computerized Patient Record System (CPRS).

Local Vista Connection: The CAPRI shortcut can be set to connect to a specific VistA system. CAPRI accepts the command line parameters s=servername and p=portname, just like CPRS.

CAPRI REMOTE: CAPRI Remote users access CAPRI through the Claims system, which runs on the VHA Forum hardware. The server for those users should be set to the claims server and address and port (Figure 1‑1). See the <u>CAPRI Remote</u> Functionality section in this user manual for more details.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/002.png)

<span id="_Toc326149538" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑1. CAPRI Desktop Shortcut Properties.

## Add/Remove Medical Centers

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Local IRM staff can set up a list of available VHA servers that the user may need to connect to on a regular basis. Please contact IRM if the user needs a facility added or removed. IRM staff can add multiple VistA connection configurations to CAPRI using the standard serverlist.exe utility provided with the Remote Procedure Call (RPC) Broker. IRM should refer to the RPC Broker documentation for specifics on how to use serverlist.exe.

If using command line parameters to direct the CAPRI shortcut to a specific server and port, the settings from serverlist.exe are ignored. The example in Step 2 below refers to a system that has been set up with serverlist.exe and has no server or port information in its shortcut.

## Logging On

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

CAPRI now uses two-factor authentication (2FA) for logging in. Your Personal Identification Verification (PIV) card along with your Personal Identification Number (PIN) is used to gain access to CAPRI. If there are issues with 2FA, the user can cancel out of the dialog that requests the user to select a certificate, and instead enter the access and verify code to log in.

### Non-CAPRI Remote Users

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Step 1 – The user starts by double-clicking the CAPRI icon.

Step 2 – OPTIONAL – If the workstation has been configured with serverlist.exe by IRM, and if there is no server and port information in the CAPRI shortcut, a window appears asking the user to select an initial server and port (Figure 1‑2). Selecting the down arrow in the upper right corner displays all the VHA sites the user can access. A scroll bar appears if the list is too long to be displayed. If the user has access to only one VHA facility, then the VistA sign on screen in Step 5 (Figure 1‑6) is displayed immediately.

Step 3 – OPTIONAL – The user scrolls to the name of the desired VHA facility, if it is not already visible, and clicks it to select it (Figure 1‑2).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/003.png)

<span id="_Toc326149539" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑2. Network Connect Dropdown Selection.

Step 4 – OPTIONAL – The user clicks <u>O</u>K (Figure 1‑3).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/004.png)

<span id="_Toc326149540" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑3. Network Connect Selection.

Step 5 – The user is prompted to select a certificate.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/005.png)

<span id="_Toc218082388" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑4. Certificate Selection

Step 6 – After selecting the correct certificate, the user is prompted to enter the PIN

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/006.png)

<span id="_Toc218082389" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑5. ActivID

Step 7 – If the 2FA fails, the user is prompted for an access/verify code. The user enters a VistA Access Code, presses the Tab key, and then enters the Verify Code. The user then presses Enter or clicks <u>O</u>K (Figure 1‑6). This takes the user to the Patient Selector screen described in the Error! Reference source not found. section.

> **NOTE:** New users without access codes should contact local IRM staff to get one.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/007.png)

<span id="_Toc326149541" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑6. VistA Sign-on Dialog Window.

### Regional Office (RO) CAPRI Remote Users

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Most VBA users are CAPRI Remote users. CAPRI Remote now uses the 2FA to connect. The users PIV card and Pin will used to gain access to authorized VA Medical Center (VAMC) sites

Step 1 – From the Start/VBAPPS/CAPRI Remote/CAPRI Remote menu, the user clicks the CAPRI icon.

Step 2 - The user is prompted to select a certificate.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/008.png)

<span id="_Toc218082391" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑7. Certificate Selection

Step 3 – After selecting the correct certificate, the user is prompted to enter the PIN

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/009.png)

<span id="_Toc218082392" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑8. ActivID

Step 4 – If 2FA fails, the user is prompted to enter an access/verify code. After entering the VistA Access Code, the user presses the Tab key to go to the next field and enters the Verify Code. Then the user presses Enter or clicks <u>O</u>K (Figure 1‑9).

> **NOTE:** New users without access codes should contact local IRM staff to get one. The first time the user logs into a VistA application, only the Access Code should be entered. CAPRI then prompt the user to create a Verify Code. Most users should have a valid Access and Verify Code combination by the time they use CAPRI.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/010.png)

<span id="_Toc326149542" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑9. VistA Sign-on Dialog Window.

OPTIONAL – To change the Verify Code, the user selects the Change Verify Code checkbox on the sign-on dialog before clicking <u>O</u>K. The user is then prompted to create a new Verify Code as shown in the steps in (Figure 1‑10)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/011.png)

<span id="_Toc326149543" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑10. Change Verify Code Option.

After selecting <u>O</u>K, the user is prompted to enter and confirm a New Verify Code (Figure 1‑11).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/012.png)

<span id="_Toc326149544" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑11. Enter New Verify Code.

Step 5 – The CAPRI Remote site selection screen (Figure 1‑12) displays the user's authorized VHA facilities. If shown, the vertical scrollbar is used to scroll through all authorized sites. The user selects a site and then either double-clicks the site's name or clicks OK to access that site. CAPRI has been modified to include the city and state where each facility is located. In addition, the list may now be sorted by State. (Figure 1‑12) shows DEV/FEX Test System in Troy, New York as the selected VHA facility.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/013.png)

<span id="_Toc326149545" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑12. Remote User Authorized Connection List.

After CAPRI loads the VHA facility, the user is prompted with the Patient Selector screen (Figure 1‑13). Instructions for use of the Patient Selector screen are found in the CAPRI – Using the Software section of this document.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/014.png)

<span id="_Toc326149546" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑13. Patient Selector Screen.

### CAPRI Alerts

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When users of the CAPRI C&P Template functionality log into CAPRI, any existing CAPRI Template Alerts are displayed (Figure 1‑18).

CAPRI automatically checks pending Compensation and Pension Worksheet Module (CPWM) Template statuses. Pending templates in the user's queue are displayed on the alert screen. The C&P Alert screen displays alerts according to template status. Alerts for template statuses are draft, awaiting signature, sent back from reviewer, requiring review, and documents to co-sign.

The user clicks the Resolve This Alert button to be taken to the section of CAPRI where the alert can be resolved. For example, if the user has unsigned templates, then they are taken to the Unsigned Templates window.

The user may select Continue to bypass the alerts and go to the Patient Selector screen.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/019.png)

<span id="_Toc326149548" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑18. CAPRI Unresolved Alerts Screen.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/020.png)

<span id="_Ref406671291" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑19. CAPRI Help Tool.

The Online Help utilizes a split screen popup window as shown in (Figure 1‑20). The left side of the window displays the Table of Contents; the right side displays a detailed description.

After logging into CAPRI, the Online Help feature is available by clicking the F1 key. The F1 key brings up context sensitive help. Alternately, the help system can be accessed via the help menu with menu item CAPRI Help F1.

This Online Help window can be minimized, enlarged to fit the screen, or closed by using the three upper right menu icons.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/021.png)

<span id="_Ref333820757" class="anchor"></span>Figure 1‑20. Navigating CAPRI Help Menu.

# CAPRI – Using the Software

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

## Patient Selector Screen or Patient Entry / Selection

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

*Security Note: The CAPRI application does not allow users to view their own personal patient records. If a user attempts to do so, CAPRI prevents access and alerts the Security Administrator at the VHA facility who takes established security violation actions. In addition, when selecting a patient who is a VA employee, CAPRI allows the user to do so only after the user agrees to Privacy Act Terms via a dialog box. An alert is sent to the Security Administrator who then inquires about the user's business reasons for accessing those records*.

### Selecting a Patient / Veteran

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The Patient Selector screen allows the user to search the VistA database for patients who have records. Patients can be located using a Social Security Number (SSN), the last name initial plus the last four digits of the SSN, or by typing in the Veteran's last name and first name. Current users of AMIE II will recognize that these are the same search methods for that application.

If the user is looking for existing VHA medical records and the patient selection search yields no results, then there are no existing VHA medical records for that patient at the facility that was accessed. Verify that the user typed in the SSN correctly and that the user is logged into the correct VHA facility.

> **NOTE:** To avoid displaying sensitive information regarding our patients and staff, the examples in this manual contain contrived names instead of real names. Patients and staff are referred to as CPRIPATIENT, ONE; PROVIDER, ONE; or USER, ONE. Likewise, real SSNs, real addresses, and other personal identifiers are not used.

Step 1 – To begin the search, the user clicks File/Select Patient and enters the Veteran's information (Figure 2‑1). For example, if the user wants to view the records of CPRIPATIENT, TWO (SSN: 666080315), then the following would be the valid search methods:

- SSN (preferred method): the user enters 666080315 and clicks Select.
- Last name initial and last four of SSN: the user enters C0315 and clicks Select.
- Name: the user enters CPRIPATIENT, TWO and clicks Select.

> **NOTE:** There are NO spaces between the comma and the first name.

Step 2 – CAPRI displays a list of possible matches (Figure 2‑1). The user can click once on the patient's name to display more information to help verify that the user has selected the correct Veteran. This additional information includes the patient's full name, SSN, claim number, gender, age, and date of birth (DOB). If there is only one match, this information is displayed automatically. After choosing the correct Veteran, the user clicks the Select button.

Step 3 – After the user clicks Select, CAPRI builds all the background information on the selected Veteran and automatically opens the C&P Exam tab. From this starting point the user can request a C&P exam for the selected Veteran or select any of the other tabs to navigate existing patient records. If there are completed DBQs within the last 12 months that have a VHA Internal DBQ Referral of "Yes" (i.e., Self-referred DBQs) on file for the patient, the list of these VHA Internal DBQ Referrals will display in order of completion date with most recent first.

> **NOTE:** For sensitive patients, this list will not display. Patients with active Category I (National) or Category II (Local) patient record flags on file in the Patient Record Flags (PRF) application will have the Record flags found message displayed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/022.png)

<span id="_Toc326149549" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑1. Selecting Patient from the Patient Selector Screen.

(Figure 2‑2) is the result screen after selecting a patient. The FLAG button will display for patients who have active National (i.e. Category I) and/or Local (i.e. Category II) Patient Record Flags recorded in the Patient Record Flags (PRF) application. The Self-Referred DBQs button will display for patients who have self-referred DBQ's completed within the last 12 months.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/023.png)

<span id="_Toc326149550" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑2. CAPRI Patient Record View.

> **NOTE:** CAPRI will display the details for the active National (i.e. Category I) and/or Local (i.e. Category II) Patient Record Flags when the user clicks the "FLAG" button:

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/024.png)

<span id="_Toc218082407" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑3. Patient Record Flags Detail.

> **NOTE:** CAPRI will display the list of any DBQs with a VHA Internal DBQ Referral of "Yes" completed within the last 12 months in order of completion date with the most recent completed DBQ first when the Self-Referred DBQs button is clicked:

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/025.png)

<span id="_Toc218082408" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑4. Self-Referred DBQs list.

Step 4 – To select another patient, the user selects File/Select Patient (Figure 2‑5) and returns to Step 1 of this section.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/026.png)

<span id="_Ref335044018" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑5. Select Patient Option.

> **NOTE:** CAPRI displays the following warning if the selected patient has active National (i.e. Category I) and/or Local (i.e. Category II) Patient Record Flags on file in the Patient Record Flag (PRF) application (Figure 2‑6).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/027.png)

<span id="_Ref43803047" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑6. Patient Record Flags Warning.

> **NOTE:** CAPRI notifies the user if the patient is deceased and allows the user to Continue or Cancel. When the user searches using the Patient Selector screen, a message indicates if the patient is deceased (Figure 2‑7).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/028.png)

<span id="_Toc326149551" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑7. Deceased Patient Notification.

The user selects <u>Y</u>es to continue or <u>N</u>o to cancel.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/029.png)

<span id="_Toc326149552" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑8. Deceased Patient Information Dialog Window.

### Sensitive Records

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

A user's own record and the records of the user's colleagues are designated as sensitive records. The user may not access them without justification. If a user clicks the name of patient whose record is sensitive, the words Sensitive Record (Figure 2‑9) are displayed in place of the patient's personal information.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/030.png)

<span id="_Toc326149553" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑9. Sensitive Record Alert.

If the user attempts to open this record, a warning message is displayed (Figure 2‑10). If the user does not have justification for viewing this record, then the user should click <u>N</u>o in the warning message.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/031.png)

<span id="_Toc326149554" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑10. Sensitive Record Warning.

### Entering a New Patient / Veteran

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

> **NOTE:** VBA users should only establish a new patient within the VistA system if it is necessary to request a C&P examination for a Veteran who is not a current patient within the medical facility's database. If the patient NAME and SSN search using CAPRI's Patient Selector function yields no results, i.e., "Match not found," then the user must establish the Veteran as a new patient.

Step 1 – The user selects the <u>E</u>nter New Pt. button at the bottom of the Patient Selector screen (Figure 2‑11).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/032.png)

<span id="_Toc326149555" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑11. Enter New Patient from Patient Selector Screen.

Step 2 – User enters the Veteran's SSN in the space provided, and then clicks <u>V</u>erify SSN is Not in Use button (Figure 2‑12).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/033.png)

<span id="_Toc326149556" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑12. Verify Veteran SSN.

Step 3 – If the SSN is not associated with an established patient record, CAPRI opens the Enter New Patient template (Figure 2‑13).

When the user selects the Click to Continue button, a query is run to find any existing patient with the following information more similar to the new patient:

- First two letters of the First and Last name are the same.
- Same last four of the SSN.
- Same Year and Month of the Birthdate.
- Same Year and Day of the Birthdate.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/034.png)<span id="_Toc326149557" class="anchor"></span>

Figure 2‑13. Enter New Patient Screen.

The query results are displayed so that the user can manually verify that the new patient does not exist in the system. The name, gender, DOB, and SSN of the potential matches are displayed, as shown in (Figure 2‑14).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/035.png)

<span id="_Toc326149558" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑14. Patient File Match.

If a sensitive record is returned as a potential match for the new patient, then the DOB and SSN fields are replaced with the word \*SENSITIVE\* (Figure 2‑15).

The user has the option to Ca<u>n</u>cel New Patient Entry or <u>C</u>ontinue With Patient Entry.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/036.png)

<span id="_Toc326149559" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑15. Sensitive Patient File Match.

Step 4 – When <u>C</u>ontinue With Patient Entry is selected, the remainder of the required fields are displayed (Figure 2‑16) and a stub record is created within the VistA system. Selecting <u>C</u>ancel at this point leaves an incomplete record.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/037.png)

<span id="_Toc326149560" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑16. Enter New Patient into VistA Patient File.

If incorrect LAST Service Entry or LAST Separation Dates are entered, the user selects the Clear Date button to remove the incorrect data from the field and then enters the correct date. There are several fields on the template with drop-down arrows. Selecting an arrow displays a menu that shows the only valid choices for each field. For example, the drop down arrow for the field Patient Type shows the following valid selections in (Figure 2‑17).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/038.png)

<span id="_Toc326149561" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑17. New Patient Type Selection.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/039.png)

<span id="_Toc326149562" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑18. New Patient Primary Eligibility.

The selection list for the Claim Folder Location (Figure 2‑19) requires the user to select the appropriate entry from the drop-down list.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/040.png)

<span id="_Toc326149563" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑19. New Patient Claim Folder Location List.

The Country field contains a dropdown list of all available countries, and defaults to UNITED STATES. This field controls the display of the ZIP+4, County, State, Province, and Postal Code fields. When the selected country is UNITED STATES, CAPRI displays the ZIP+4, County, and State fields but hides the Province and Postal Code fields (Figure 2‑20)

After selecting UNITED STATES in the Country field, a user entering a valid ZIP code into the Zip+4 field causes CAPRI to populate the City, County, and State fields with predetermined, un-editable values. The City, County, and State field values may only be edited by a user who possesses the EAS GMT COUNTY EDIT security key.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/041.png)

<span id="_Toc326149564" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑20. New Patient Location Record.

When the selected Country is other than UNITED STATES, CAPRI displays the Province, and Postal Code fields and hides the ZIP+4, County, and State fields (Figure 2‑21).

Step 5 – The user selects <u>A</u>dd Patient to establish the new patient or <u>C</u>ancel if the user no longer wishes to add the patient shown in (Figure 2‑16) and (Figure 2‑21).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/042.png)

<span id="_Toc326149565" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑21. New Patient Location

Notes on adding new patients:

1.  CAPRI automatically uses all capital letters in all fields; users do not have to capitalize individual letters.
2.  Do not use punctuation marks, special characters, or spaces in any of the name fields.
3.  Do not use punctuation marks in any of the address fields.
4.  Use a hyphen between the area code and number, e.g., 555-555-5555; Do not put parentheses around the area code of a phone number.

Step 6 – After the user has completed the Enter New Patient template and clicked the Add Patient button shown in ( Figure 2‑16) and (Figure 2‑21), CAPRI builds a new patient record for the Veteran and automatically opens that record in the C&P Exams tab.

Step 7 – The new patient is established in VistA and the user may now request a C&P Examination for that patient.

### Other Facilities Visited

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

A CAPRI remote user can switch to other VAMC sites and view the patient's record even if the user normally does not have access to those VAMC sites. The Other Facilities Visited button allows a user to identify all VAMCs where a patient *may have* visited and identifies the last time the patient *may have* received treatment at that VAMC site. The Other Facilities Visited button on the Patient Selector screen is grayed out and disabled when there is no electronic record that the patient has visited another VAMC site (Figure 2‑22).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/043.png)

<span id="_Toc326149566" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑22. Other Facilities Visited.

When a Veteran's patient record is displayed, the Other Facilities Visited button is in the upper left corner (Figure 2‑23).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/044.png)

<span id="_Toc326149567" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑23. Other Facilities Visited Selection.

After selecting the Other Facilities Visited button from the Patient Selector screen (Figure 2‑22), or the Other Facilities Visited button while viewing a named Veteran's records (Figure 2‑23), the user is presented with a list of VAMCs where the Veteran *may have* visited (Figure 2‑24). The user selects a VAMC, and then selects <u>S</u>witch to This Facility.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/045.png)

<span id="_Toc326149568" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑24. Remote Sites Visited List.

After switching to the other site, the user has full ability to navigate the Veteran's patient records using CAPRI.

Unless the user has direct access to the switched site, the file menu cannot be used to search for other patient records at the switched site. Instead, the user receives the message in (Figure 2‑25).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/046.png)

<span id="_Toc326149569" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑25. Insufficient Access Alert Window.

### Enterprise Patient/Veteran Search Function

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The Enterprise Search Function uses Master Veteran Index (MVI) as the search database to retrieve a list of sites where patient records exists. This feature allows the user to enter traits of a patient (see Figure 2‑26 to see if a match exists. This functionality is available from the Tools menu.

#### Search Function

The following screenshot (Figure 2‑26) is from the Patient Selector screen. To initiate a search, click either the Enterprise Search button (Figure 2‑26) or select Enterprise Search for a Patient from the Tools menu (Figure 2‑27).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/047.png)

<span id="_Ref406671620" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑26. Enterprise Search Selection.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/048.png)

<span id="_Ref406687640" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑27. Enterprise Search from Tools Menu Selection.

The Enterprise Search input form (Figure 2‑28) allows the traits of a patient to be entered. The Person Traits are as follows:

- First Name (Required)
- Middle Name (Optional)
- Last Name (Required)
- Social Security Number (Required)
- Date of Birth (Required)

The results from the Person Search displays in the Search Result memo box on the Enterprise Search form are processed depending on the results as follows:

- One exact match –The patient's person traits and the treating facilities with station number is displayed. Double click on that treating facility to display patient information from that facility.
- No match – If no match is found a "No match found for entered person traits" will be displayed.
- Multiple matches – The patient's person traits are displayed, and the user is allowed to select the desired record. The treating facilities with station number displays for the selected patient. Double click on a selected treating facility to display patient information for that specific treating facility.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/049.png)

<span id="_Ref334950368" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑28. Enterprise Search Dialog Window.

#### "Legacy" Enterprise Search Functionality

The "Legacy" Enterprise Search button can be used to search all VAMC VistA systems to see where a patient has presented for care. This function allows the user to enter a combination of patient name and SSN to search through selected VAMC systems to see if a patient match exists. To initiate a "Legacy" Enterprise search, click the Legacy Searchbutton.

The following (Figure 2‑29) is still available from the Legacy Searchbutton.

Step 1– Next to the Search For: field, the user may click the "?" button to receive instructions for entering specified search parameters.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/050.png)

<span id="_Toc326149571" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑29. Legacy Search Help Button.

> **NOTE:** The search string should be the same text VistA or CAPRI would normally use for searching the patient file.

Examples:

- Enter an SSN without the dashes: 123456789 instead of 123-45-6789
- Enter last name + comma + first name with no spaces: "DOE,JOHN" instead of "DOE, JOHN".
- Enter first letter of last name + last 4 digits of SSN: A1234

> **NOTE:** There is a limit of 50 items returned per search, per site.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/051.png)

<span id="_Toc326149572" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑30. Legacy Search Help Instructions Dialog Window.

Step 2 – Single site, all sites, multiple sites, or a group of site searches can be done from the screens shown in (Figure 2‑31), (Figure 2‑32), (Figure 2‑33) and (Figure 2‑34), respectively.

#### Searching One (Single) Site

Single Site: After entering patient search data, the user selects one site and then clicks the <u>R</u>unSelected button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/052.png)

<span id="_Toc326149573" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑31. Single Site Run Search Selection.

#### Searching All Sites in List

After entering patient search data, the user clicks the Select All button, and then clicks <u>R</u>un Selected Button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/053.png)

<span id="_Toc326149574" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑32. All Site Run Search Selection.

#### Searching Multiple Sites

After entering patient search data, to select multiple sites, select one site with the first mouse-click and then hold the Control (CTRL) key on the keyboard while selecting the names of other sites. After choosing all desired sites, click <u>R</u>un Selected (Figure 2‑33).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/054.png)

<span id="_Toc326149575" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑33. Multiple Site Search Selection.

#### Searching a Group of Sites in the List

After entering patient search data, the user selects one site, holds the Shift key on the keyboard, and clicks the last site in the grouping search. Then, the user clicks <u>R</u>un Selected (Figure 2‑34).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/055.png)

<span id="_Toc326149576" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑34. Group Site Search Selection.

Step 3 – Potential Matches received after running a selected search parameter are shown in (Figure 2‑35). The user can move the vertical scroll bar to see the complete list.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/056.png)

<span id="_Toc326149577" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑35. Site Search Results.

If CAPRI is unable to connect to a selected site that information is reported (Figure 2‑36).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/057.png)

<span id="_Toc326149578" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑36. Failed Connection Search Result.

## C&P Exam Requests

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The C&P Exam tab (Figure 2‑37) includes functions such as:

- Requesting C&P examinations
- Viewing/editing requests
- Canceling requests
- Re-routing a request
- Adding an exam to a pending request
- Creating status inquiry reports
- Viewing completed requests
- Tracking the progress of the request for claims management purposes
- Requesting an Insufficient Exam
- Printing results for individual patients

Pending requests are shown with a date only in the left column. Completed requests have a completion date in the right column. When the user first accesses this screen, none of the examinations are selected, and only the Add a New Request button is enabled. After the user selects an examination, the Re-Print Final C&P Results, Status Inquiry, and View/Edit Selected Request buttons are enabled.

### View/Edit Selected Request

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the C&P Exams tab (Figure 2‑37). If there are no examination requests, the window is blank. If the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested. The user can sort the Date Requested/Date Completed by ascending or descending order. Ascending (oldest to newest) is the default.

Step 2 – The user selects the examination request date and clicks the View/Edit Selected Re<u>q</u>uest button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/058.png)

<span id="_Ref333174600" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑37. View/Edit Selected Request.

Step 3 – The View C&P screen opens, showing all the entries from the original request, as well the examination status (Figure 2‑38). The user can scroll down to see additional information from the original request. The exam in this request cannot be edited because it is already complete, but the original request may be viewed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/059.png)

<span id="_Toc326149580" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑38. C&P Exam Selection Results Screen.

Step 4 – If the user wants to edit a request that is still pending and has not yet been scheduled, it can be done on the View C&P Exam screen shown in (Figure 2‑38). Otherwise, skip to Step 8. The user can edit Claim Folder Required?Priority of Exam(s), Comments, and Other Disabilities by typing directly into those fields. To add another examination, the user clicks the <u>A</u>dd Exam to Request button shown in (Figure 2‑39).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/060.png)

<span id="_Toc326149581" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑39. View Selected Exam Selection.

Step 5 – If the <u>A</u>dd Exam to Request button is clicked, the exam listing field opens allowing the user to make a selection (Figure 2‑40). The user can scroll down the list or use the Find by Body Syste<u>m</u> button. In the Find by Body Syste<u>m</u> view (Figure 2‑40), the user scrolls down the body systems until the correct one is found, then single-click the "+" box in front of the body system name, or the user can double-click the body system name to open a list of all pertinent examinations. The user double-clicks the desired examination to add it. The view reverts to the scroll list view, and that examination is checked.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/061.png)

<span id="_Toc326149582" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑40. Add Exam List.

Step 6 – In the scroll list view (Figure 2‑41Error! Reference source not found.), the user clicks the checkbox next to any of the desired examination.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/062.png)

<span id="_Ref203487881" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑41. Add Exam List – Scroll View.

Step 7 – The user selects <u>A</u>dd Selected Exams or Ca<u>n</u>cel Add Exams as appropriate (Figure 2‑40)

Step 8 – The user can select Close Window to close this screen or Add An Insufficient Exam Request if this examination was completed but is insufficient. See the Add a New Request section for additional information.

### Cancel an Exam Request

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Step 1 – After logging into CAPRI, the user looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector and moves to the C&P Exams tab (Figure 2‑37). If there are no examination requests, the window is blank. If the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested.

Step 2 – The user selects the examination request date and clicks the View/Edit Selected Request button (Figure 2‑37).

Step 3 – The View C&P Exam screen opens, showing all the entries from the original request and the examination status (Figure 2‑42). The user can scroll down to see additional information from the original request.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/063.png)

<span id="_Toc326149584" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑42. View C&P Exam Screen.

Step 4 – To cancel all exams, the user scrolls down to the Exams Requested section and clicks the Cancel ALL Exams button (Figure 2‑43). To cancel selected exams, skip to Step 6.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/064.png)

<span id="_Toc326149585" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑43. Cancel All Exams Selection.

Step 5 – CAPRI opens a dialog box for the user to confirm the selection (Figure 2‑44). The user clicks <u>Y</u>es to cancel the exams or <u>N</u>o if the user still wants the exams to be completed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/065.png)

<span id="_Toc326149586" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑44. Cancel All Exams Alert.

Step 6 – To cancel selected exams, the user scrolls down to the Exams Requested section (Figure 2‑45), clicks the exam to be canceled, and then clicks the <u>V</u>iew Selected Exam button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/066.png)

<span id="_Toc326149587" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑45. Cancel Single Exam.

Step 7 – The C&P Exam Details screen opens (Figure 2‑46). The user can review the exam request to ensure that the correct exam is selected before clicking the Ca<u>n</u>cel this Exam button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/067.png)

<span id="_Toc326149588" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑46. C&P Exam Details Screen.

Step 8 – A box opens for the user to select the cancellation reason from the supplied list (Figure 2‑47). The <u>O</u>K button is disabled until a reason is selected. After selecting the appropriate reason, the user clicks <u>O</u>K to cancel the selected exam. If the user no longer wishes to cancel the exam, then the user selects <u>A</u>bort and DO NOTCANCEL! to keep the exam.

NOTE If a user is logged in to a local VistA system, Cancel by MAS, is enabled. If user is a remote user (CLAIMS system), Cancel By Regional Office is enabled.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/068.png)

<span id="_Toc326149589" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑47. Cancel Exam Reason Screen.

Step 9 – If <u>O</u>K was selected to cancel the exam request, CAPRI displays a confirmation box (Figure 2‑48). The user can click <u>Y</u>es to cancel the exam or <u>N</u>o to keep the exam.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/069.png)

<span id="_Toc326149590" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑48. Cancel Exam Confirmation Alert.

Step 10 – If <u>Y</u>es is selected, CAPRI opens a text field. The user may use this to enter comments that will be sent with the cancellation message to VHA (Figure 2‑49). After entering comments, click <u>D</u>one.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/070.png)

<span id="_Toc326149591" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑49. Cancel Exam Comments.

Step 11 – The C&P Exams tab now shows the canceled exam (Figure 2‑50).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/071.png)

<span id="_Toc326149592" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑50. Confirm Canceled Exam.

### Add a New Request

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the C&P Exams tab (Figure 2‑37). If there are no examination requests, the window is blank. If the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested.

Step 2 – If previous examination requests exist, the user views those requests as described in View/Edit Selected Request to ensure that the user is not about to enter a duplicate request for an examination.

Step 3 – After ensuring that the exam about to be requested is not already pending, the user clicks the Add a New Request button (Figure 2‑37).

Step 4 – CAPRI prompts the user to check the latest address of record in the VistA system (Figure 2‑51). If the Veteran has a new address, the user clicks the <u>E</u>dit Address Now button. If the user is not sure if the new address is more current than the one shown by CAPRI, then the Permanent Address is not updated and instead this fact is noted, and the new address is included in the general comments area of the exam request.

If the Permanent Address: shown by CAPRI is current, the user clicks <u>O</u>K. If the user enters a change to the Permanent Address: information, it does not update the address information directly in the VistA Registration database; instead, this information is appended as text to the examination request, where it can be further edited if necessary.

> **NOTE:** VBA users cannot edit the Temporary Address information in (Figure 2‑51).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/072.png)

<span id="_Toc326149593" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑51. Address Verification Screen

Step 5 – If the user selects the <u>E</u>dit Address Now button, then the address can be edited. The user has the option of either saving or not saving the address changes to the C&P Exam request (Figure 2‑52).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/073.png)

<span id="_Toc326149594" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑52. Address Verification Screen—Save Address.

After the user selects <u>O</u>K from (Figure 2‑51), or either the <u>D</u>on't Save button or the <u>S</u>ave Changes With C&P Request button from (Figure 2‑52), an Add a New C&P Exam dialog box opens (Figure 2‑53

As shown in (Figure 2‑53), certain VHA facilities have added new examination list functionality. For these facilities only, supported exams are shown in black, and exams shown in red may not be supported by the selected facility. and Information about this Exam List status messages have been added to the template. Pop-up messages may appear during exam selection, depending on how the exam list has been set up by the C&P clinic or MAS. You can use the Tab key or the mouse to navigate among the fields. Several fields have drop-down arrows which show the valid selections for that particular field. All fields except for Other Disabilities require an entry. The Other Disabilities fields are not used by all VBA Regional Offices.

Note 1: If the user did not use the Other Disabilities fields with the AMIE software, then they are not used with CAPRI.

Note 2: CAPRI includes the following Claim Types from the dropdown list:

- BDD
- IDES
- INCREASE ONLY
- ORIGINAL
- QUICK START
- SUPPLEMENTAL

Note 3: CAPRI includes the following Special Considerations

- AGE OF CLAIM
- FDC
- INCARCERATED
- HOMELESS
- NOT APPLICABLE
- POW
- PURPLE HEART
- TERMINAL
- VICAP

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/074.png)

<span id="_Toc326149595" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑53. Add New C&P Exam Screen.

Note 3: CAPRI has been modified to default to the Last Rating Exam Date field with N/A. If the user chooses a date and decides to delete it they must click anywhere in the field and click the Delete key on the keyboard and they are prompted with the following message shown in (Figure 2‑54) and the field then is changed back to N/A if they choose <u>Y</u>es to the message. If they choose <u>N</u>o it remains with the previously chosen date*.*

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/075.png)

<span id="_Toc326149596" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑54. Delete Exam Date Alert.

The user can select as many Exams to Complete as required by the Veteran's claim. The user must remember to justify the request by entering comments that explain the choice of exams. The Exams to Complete section work the same way as described in Step 5 and Step 6 of the View/Edit Selected Request section.

Step 6 – The user enters comments into the Comments field with information for the C&P physician concerning the Veteran's disabilities along with the reason for the user's selection of examinations (Figure 2‑53). The Comments area is Windows-compatible and supports copy and paste commands. The user carries out copy and paste commands by either selecting Edit, located in the gray bar at the top of the screen, or by right-clicking in the comments area. The copy and paste features are of benefit for lengthy VBA Remand instructions for C&P examinations and also for those Ratings Veterans Services Representatives (RSVRs) and Data Request Outputs (DROs) that keep a card file or other electronic system of exam paragraphs with standardized sentences that are used on a frequent basis. Additionally, the C&P medical opinion request template can be pasted into the Comments section.

The Exam Request Template button was added to automate some of these paragraphs. It opens another screen (Figure 2‑55) which is an optional feature that allows the user to make selections that use standard language and appear on the examination request. It has tabs for Info, SC/Increased Eval, Pension, Medical Opinion, and Contact Info. Use of these templates is optional. The Info tab has general information for the examiner (Figure 2‑55).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/076.png)

<span id="_Toc326149597" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑55. Exam Request Template—Info Tab.

The SC/Increased Eval tab allows the user to specify a Power of Attorney, indicate the conditions for which the Veteran is claiming service connection, and indicate the service connected conditions for which the Veteran is claiming an increased evaluation. Selecting the Add New button opens the bottom Medical Condition fields (Figure 2‑56).

Note 4: When a medical opinion is required for a claim of service connection, DO NOT use the Veteran claims service connection for: selection. Instead, refer to the VBA M21-1, MR for guidance as to what is required.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/077.png)

<span id="_Toc326149598" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑56. Exam Request Template—SC/Increased Eval Tab.

On the Pension tab, the user indicates what medical conditions must be evaluated for a determination of pension entitlement (Figure 2‑57).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/078.png)

<span id="_Toc326149599" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑57. Exam Request Template—Pension Tab.

The Medical Opinion tab allows the user to indicate that an opinion is needed (Figure 2‑58). The user can copy and paste text into this box by pointing to it and clicking the right mouse button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/079.png)

<span id="_Toc326149600" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑58. Exam Request Template—Medical Opinion Tab.

The Contact Info tab gives the user a pre-formatted area to indicate a station contact if the examiner has any questions about the request (Figure 2‑59). CAPRI remembers this information and automatically populates these fields the next time this template is used.

The user clicks Done when finished to enter all of the applicable templates.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/080.png)

<span id="_Toc326149601" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑59. Exam Request Template—Contact Info Tab.

Step 7 – The user reviews the request to ensure all the correct information has been entered. After making sure that no additional information is required, the user clicks Send Exam Request. The user may also click Cancel Request, if appropriate, and answer the confirmation dialog box that appears.

Step 8 – The request is transmitted to the VHA facility and CAPRI shows that the request is pending.

Step 9 – To place proof of the exam request in the claim file for record keeping purposes, the user selects the Status Inquiry option and prints the Inquiry screen.

### Insufficient Exam Request

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

This option is used if a completed examination is not sufficient, and the RO has followed local procedures to attempt to make the examination sufficient for rating purposes.

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the C&P Exams tab (Figure 2‑60). When the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested and completed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/081.png)

<span id="_Toc326149602" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑60. C&P Exam Requests.

Step 2 – The user selects the previous examination that was insufficient, and then clicks View/Edit Selected Request. This opens the View C&P Exam screen.

> **NOTE:** The request status can be either "RELEASED TO RO, NOT PRINTED" or "COMPLETED, PRINTED BY RO" to mark insufficient.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/082.png)

<span id="_Toc326149603" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑61. View C&P Exam—Insufficient Exam View.

(Figure 2‑62) shows the previously requested exams, and that their status is complete.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/083.png)

<span id="_Toc326149604" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑62. View C&P Exam—Completed Exams.

Step 3 – The user verifies that the correct request was selected, and then clicks the <u>A</u>dd An Insufficient Exam Request button (Figure 2‑61).

Step 4 – The Add New Insufficient C&P Exam screen opens and defaults the Insufficient field to "YES," which is not editable by the user. (Figure 2‑53). The user completes this screen as directed in Section 2.2.3, Add a New Request. The only difference is that the list of available examinations is limited to those completed in the previous, insufficient examination (Figure 2‑63). These choices only appear after the user enters the Routing Location.

> **NOTE:** If for some reason the exam that was previously completed is no longer active in the AMIE Exam file, the entire list of exams that are active will be displayed to the user.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/084.png)

<span id="_Toc326149605" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑63. Add New Insufficient C&P Exam Screen.

Step 5 – The user chooses the exam(s) to mark insufficient and clicks the "Send Exam Request" button.

Step 6 – The user then chooses an "Insufficient Reason" from the "Select an insufficiency reason and click "OK" section and enters any comments in the "Insufficiency Comments" window and clicks "OK" and click "YES" to "Are you sure you want to send this new 2507 request?"

If the user decides not to mark insufficient, they can choose the "Abort and DO NOT MARK INSUFFICIENT" button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/085.png)

<span id="_Toc218082468" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑64. Add New Insufficient C&P Exam Screen—Select Reason.

This option is used to display final C&P examination results. The user can print the results, if needed, by using File/Print after the results are displayed.

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the C&P Exams tab. If there are no examination requests, the window is blank. If the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested, as in the example in (Figure 2‑65) below.

This option is used to check the status of any exam request shown on the C&P Exam tab screen.

If the Veteran already has examination requests on file, the window shows the dates requested, as in the example below.
