## Compensation and Pension Worksheets

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Overview

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The C&P Worksheets tab (Figure 2‑150) provides access to a defined set of point-and-click templates used for documenting a Veteran's C&P examinations. A new PDF template is Disability Beneifit Questionnairre (DBQ) in PDF format. A DBQ is completed for each contention associated to the C&P exam.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/172.png)

<span id="_Toc326149668" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑150. C&P Worksheets Tab.

Users who need access to this tab in CAPRI must have the appropriate Security Key allocation before the tab is visible and functions become active. (See the following section "User Setup"). VBA users are typically unable to access this tab and are prevented from seeing a document before it is signed and finalized by the exam provider.

### User Setup to access C&P Worksheet Tabs

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

#### Security Key Assignment 

To activate Compensation and Pension Worksheet Module (CPWM) for a particular user, a menu option and several Security Keys must be assigned. Information Resource Management (IRM) needs to coordinate with the C&P clinic to determine what keys a user should receive. Most users need two new keys. The reviewer type user needs three keys.

DVBA CAPRI CLIN DOC-EFOLDER – This Security Key allows users to send clinical documents to VBA eFolder.

DVBA CLINICAL METRICS PURGE – This Security Key allows users to purge site transmission metrics.

DVBA CAPRI GUI – This is the <u>option context</u> to the main CAPRI application. All CAPRI users need to be assigned access to this menu option.

DVBA CAPRI WORKSHEET TAB – This <u>Security Key</u> makes the C&P Worksheets tab visible inside CAPRI. All VHA CAPRI users should get this Security Key.

Some sites require the exam to be sent to a reviewer when it is complete. One and only one of the following <u>Security Keys</u> must be assigned for anyone who is creating new C&P exam templates with CPWM.

1\. DVBAB CPWM DISALLOW REVIEW – User does not need documents reviewed prior to release.

2\. DVBAB CPWM OPTIONAL REVIEW – User can choose to send some documents for review and no other documents.

3\. DVBAB CPWM REQUIRE REVIEW – User must have all documents reviewed by a reviewer prior to upload.

OPTIONALSecurityKey – If the site chooses to use the review process, users designated as reviewers must be assigned the DVBAB CPWM REVIEWER Security Key.

It is imperative that users not be given every DVBA\* key due to the keys having conflicting uses and the probability of unexpected results. If users are unsure of which Security Key to assign, they should contact the National VistA Helpdesk.

#### Site Considerations: Review Functionality

Before implementing CPWM, sites should consider the business roles of each user who is accessing the system. The CPWM module can be configured several ways, depending on site preference and business needs. Each site should carefully consider the following configuration options. Examinations can be completed through one of the following paths:

1\. If the provider is allowed to Sign and release templates to the RO upon their completion without review by another party, then the IRM person should assign them Security Key DVBAB CPWM DISALLOW REVIEW.

2\. If the provider needs to send templates to a reviewer for approval before completion, then the IRM person should assign them Security Key DVBAB CPWM REQUIRE REVIEW.

3\. If the provider has the option to send the template to a reviewer or sign and release immediately, then the IRM person should assign them Security Key DVBAB CPWM OPTIONAL REVIEW.

The following figures demonstrate what a user sees at the bottom of the screen when working with a CPWM document with the assigned Security Key.

1.  DVBAB CPWM DISALLOW REVIEW – Assignment of this Security Key results in the Send for <u>R</u>eview for Signature and <u>S</u>end for Review buttons being disabled, as shown in (Figure 2‑151).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/173.png)

<span id="_Toc326149669" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑151. CPWM—Send for Signature and Send for Review Disabled.

2.  DVBAB CPWM OPTIONAL REVIEW – Assignment of this Security Key results in the user being able to choose whether to Send for <u>R</u>eview or select <u>D</u>one! and <u>S</u>end for Signature being disabled as shown in (Figure 2‑152)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/174.png)

<span id="_Toc326149670" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑152. CPWM—Send for Review Enabled and Send for Signature Disabled.

3.  DVBAB CPWM REQUIRE REVIEW – All of the user's documents require a review prior to going into AMIE and Text Integration Utilities (TIU). Assignment of this Security Key results in the Send for <u>R</u>eview is enabled while <u>D</u>one! and <u>S</u>end for Signature are disabled are shown in (Figure 2‑153).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/175.png)

<span id="_Toc326149671" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑153. CPWM—Send for Review Enabled and Send for Signature disabled.

When another user attempts to open a worksheet without the correct assigned security privileges, the following error message will display (Figure 2‑154).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/176.png)

<span id="_Ref164324275" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑154 Worksheet in security privileges message.

### C&P Worksheet Tab Functionalities

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

#### Worksheet Status

The C&P Worksheets tab contains the list of pending and completed worksheets for the selected patient, if any exist. Information displayed includes template (worksheet) name, author, worksheet status, and the date the worksheet data was sent to CPRS (where applicable). The worksheet status value is one of the following:

- Draft/Not Ready – started but not completed.
- ReviewPending – sent for review.
- Signed – DBQ has been signed.
- SentBack – sent back to the clinician by the reviewer for changes.
- Complete – completed and the data sent to CPRS on the date shown.
- Uncosigned – signed by the author but the author requires a cosigner in CAPRI.
- Awaiting Signature – waiting for a provider to sign the template.
- Outdated Template – one or more DBQs are not using the current DBQ template version.
- Permanent Failure – DBQs that are unable to be re-transmitted. (See Section [3.16](#permanent-failure-dbq-transmission-error-message) for more information)
- Transmission Error – issue during transmission. (See Section [3.17](#dbq-transmission-error-message) for more information)

#### Opening a CPWM Exam

The user may open a pending template in the C&P Worksheet tab display by selecting it and selecting the Display PDF Template button, or by double-clicking the name of the desired template. All templates with Complete status are viewable, however a user may view only their own unsigned templates without Reviewer access.

Users will not be able to view, edit, or delete a worksheet if there is another user with the same worksheet open. The following message will display when attempting to open a worksheet that is locked (Figure 2‑155).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/177.png)

<span id="_Ref171072103" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑155 Current Worksheet Locked Message

If the user is unable to unlock a worksheet, the user will receive the following error message (Figure 2‑156).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/178.png)

<span id="_Ref184711400" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑156 Unlock Worksheet Failed Message

When opening a template, if outdated, a message will display (Figure 2‑157) informing the user an update to the template has been made and to verify all user input fields before saving, as the fields may have changed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/179.png)

<span id="_Ref138926694" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑157 IEPD Version of Worksheet Has Changed

Each time a DBQ template is opened within the CAPRI GUI, an IEPD version number comparison is executed between the IEPD version stored in the parameter file and the IEPD version of the open worksheet.

If template is found to be outdated, user input fields stored in VistA will map to the appropriate fields in the new PDF version.

A message is displayed to the user informing them an update has been made and requesting a validation of all user input fields before saving.

#### Deleting a CPWM Exam

The user may delete any pending template in the C&P Worksheet tab display by selecting it and clicking Delete PDF Template. The user is then prompted to verify the deletion by entering the number displayed in the verification window. The user can delete their template only without Reviewer access. Templates with a status of Complete cannot be deleted.

#### Copying an existing CPWM Exam

To copy a pending CPWM template, the user selects the template name on the list and then selects the Copy PDF Template button. The system displays a prompt "Would you like a NEW copy of the selected template and assign it to yourself?" After the user selects Yes, the template is copied and assigned to the current user. The copied template moves to a Draft/Not Ready status.

#### Refresh the List

The user may update the C&P Worksheet tab display for the selected patient by selecting Refresh List. The refresh function normally happens automatically, but it may be performed manually as well.

#### Flagging

Three visual flags are available on the C&P Worksheets tab and the unsigned templates dialog box. Exam templates that are "new" have an "N" flag. After it is viewed, the flag clears automatically. A green flag and red exclamation mark are available for use according to user needs. These flags can be toggled on and off by right clicking any template name displayed on the C&P worksheets tab or unsigned template dialog box, then selecting toggle on/off.

#### Creating a New C&P Exam

To create a new C&P exam, the user clicks the New PDF Template button (Figure 2‑158). The user must have the required Security Key to create a new worksheet.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/180.png)

<span id="_Toc326149672" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑158. New Template Button.

The Browse Templates screen is displayed (Figure 2‑159).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/181.png)

<span id="_Toc326149673" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑159. Browse Template Screen.

Step 1 – The user may select a single template or multiple templates by double-clicking the desired templates from the Available Templates: list box, or by selecting a template and clicking the <u>A</u>dd this template button. The templates that make up the exam appear in the Selected Templates: list box. If the user selects multiple templates, the system merges the selected forms into a single form. The multiple exams will appear as separate tabs when viewed.

Description: field displays information about the template version and the last date that the template was updated.

Once the template(s) are selected, press the Generate Template button. (Figure 2‑159.) Shows the merged templates, one tab for each exam.)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/182.png)

<span id="_Ref203487927" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑160. Merged Form Screen.

Step 2 – The user must select a value of "No" or "Yes" for the VHA Internal DBQ Referral flag on the top of each form (Figure 2‑160). Selecting NO means that the report is a C&P evaluation in response to a request (2507) requested by the VBA. Selecting YES means that the DBQ is part of the DBQ Referral program (i.e. Self-Referred)

Step 3 – The user enters data on the form by selecting the tabs and completing the fields, which are specific to the selected templates.

- CAPRI DBQ PDF template viewer supports tabbing through all DBQ field types in the sequence order defined within the PDF file in order to be 508 compliant. Users can move forward between fields by pressing the Tab key and move backward by pressing Shift + Tab. Within radio groups, the arrow keys are used to change selections, and the Enter key is used to select or unselect options. Conditional logic is also supported: when options such as Yes/No are selected or unselected, any dependent (child) fields are automatically reset, just as they would be in Adobe Acrobat.
- Radio buttons in DBQ PDFs function like those in standard PDF viewers; once an option is selected, it cannot be unselected directly. To clear a selected radio button, select the radio group and press F12, which will clear and reset the radio buttons in that group.
- In the "Date of Examination" field within a DBQ PDF, you are required to manually enter a date for when the examination occurs. Future dates are not valid and the date entered must be in the following format: MM/DD/YYYY.

When you click Done on the DBQ PDF, validation occurs for the Date of Examination field. If the date format does not match the MM/DD/YYYY format or is a future date, a warning notification is displayed so you can correct it.

> **NOTE:** You are not required to enter a leading zero for single numeral months or days.

If there are required fields on the template that are not completed, the user is notified either while using the form or prior to signature. To review what was entered, the user clicks the Preview button.(Figure 2‑161)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/183.png)

<span id="_Ref207791251" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑161. PDF Print Preview

When the data entry for the exam is completed, the next step in processing the template is performed based on the Security Key assigned to the user.

- No review process – Done is active, Send for Review is deactivated.
- Review process optional – Done is active, Send for Review is active.
- Review process required – Done is inactive, Send for Review is active.

For the reviewer, when the template is in a Review Pending status, the user has the option to send for signature or to select <u>D</u>one to complete the template unless the template being utilized is outdated (See Section 2.10.5 Outdated Template Check). Selecting <u>S</u>end for Signature allows the user to designate the signer.

(Possible) Step 4 – If the <u>D</u>one button is clicked, and one of the options from the VHA Internal DBQ Referral Yes/No drop-down has not been selected, a warning message will appear and the DBQ will not be completed (Figure 2‑162).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/184.png)

<span id="_Ref79657571" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑162. Required Field Warning Message.

Step 5- If an Ok option has been selected, the user will be asked if the report was performed using ACE (Figure 2-162).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/185.png)

<span id="_Toc218082567" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑163. ACE Confirmation

Step 6 – After <u>Y</u>es or <u>N</u>o is selected, the Signature Validation screen is displayed (Figure 2-163) and the user will be required to select an appointment, select an admission date, or specify that there was no appointment. Note that CAPRI has been modified to prevent displaying appointments with dates in the future from appearing in the list of Appointments. After a selection is made, the remainder of the fields on the Signature Validation screen are displayed.

> **NOTE:** For instruction on completing a "NON-C&P Exam" refer to Section 2.9.3.10 below.

> ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/186.png)

> <span id="_Toc218082568" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑164. Signature Validation Screen.

> Exam requests that have been re-routed and not accepted (New, Re-Routed, or Re-Routed, Pending Acceptance) will receive a warning message when trying to link in an open exam to the request (Figure 2‑165). This will block the user from completing the exam.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/187.png)

<span id="_Ref79656623" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑165. Signature Validation Warning Message for Re-Routed Requests

TITLE: Title selection is required. The dropdown list for titles displays only titles that contain the words "C&P" or "Compensation and Pension" in the title name.

EXPECTED COSIGNER: Users may require a cosigner based on their clinical privileges to designate a cosigner for a user, IRM or the local Clinical Applications Coordinator (CAC) should be contacted. There are no cosigner-specific keys or setup unique to CAPRI.

> **NOTE:** The basic rule is that a title-requiring a co-signature in CPRS requires a cosigner in CAPRI as well.

PRE or POST DISCHARGE:

- From the drop-down menu, select Pre Discharge or Post Discharge.
- Select Pre Discharge if the patient's proposed date of separation from active service is ON or AFTER the date of the examination being performed.
- Select Post Discharge if the patient's last date of separation from active service was BEFORE the date of the examination being performed.
- Click the Details button for a description of Pre or Post Discharge.

> **NOTE:** The CAPRI Signature Validation screen will automatically default to Post Discharge. The clinician MUST change the selection to Pre-Discharge when applicable.

#### Invalid Electronic Signature

If the user enters an invalid electronic signature, an error message is displayed (Figure 2-165).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/188.png)

<span id="_Toc218082570" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑166. Invalid Electronic Signature Alert.

After the user clicks OK, they are returned to the ELECTRONIC SIG. CODE: field on the Signature Validation form.

Valid Electronic Signature: After CAPRI has validated the electronic signature code, a progress note record is created. The user must select the correct request under Requests Available. A list of the open exams for that request is displayed. Note that all open exams must be cancelled or completed before the report can be released.

Only examinations containing no exam report are offered as selections under Open Exams after selecting an open AMIE exam request. If an expected exam is not listed, it might be because the exam already has either a partial or a complete transcription. The transcription can be removed by the appropriate clinic staff and CAPRI displays that exam request as a selection.

If there is an open request but no exams are available, then all requested exams have been populated with either a template or transcription, but the request has not yet been released. The request needs to be released through either CAPRI or AMIE by clerical or supervisory staff.

When an examiner completes an exam from the signature validation screen it will automatically be transmitted to VLER DAS.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/189.png)

<span id="_Toc218082571" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑167. Transmitting XML to DAS Dialog.

The user will not have to acknowledge transmission. If transmissions to VLER DAS fails, CAPRI will automatically reattempt to transmit up to five times (Figure 2‑168). If the fifth attempt fails, the user will receive an error message (Figure 2‑169) and an XML file for the Disability Benefits Questionnaire (DBQ) is saved and can be retransmitted using the VBA eFolder Queue button at a later time.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/190.png)

<span id="_Ref34998092" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑168. VLER Re-Transmit Status.

> **NOTE:** DBQs transmitted to VBMS will conform to the IEPD Schema 1.3 or 2.x depending on the CAPRI properties established at the time of transmission.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/191.png)

<span id="_Ref34998217" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑169. VLER Transmission Failed Message.

<span id="_Completing_a_Non" class="anchor"></span>NOTE: Exam Results will be stored as a progress note "only" and will not be automatically shared with VBA.

If a non-C&P exam is sent for co-signature, please note that this note will be marked complete and will "not" be displayed in the "Co-Signature Alert Resolution" screen.

Non-C&P exams will "not" be transmitted to VBA eFolder or Data Access Services (DAS).

#### Viewing a Signed DBQ

Signed DBQs can be viewed in read-only mode within the C&P Worksheets tab. To view a signed DBQ, select the DBQ Template Name and double click. The DBQ will launch in a separate window (Figure 2-169).

The window will display the DBQ in read-only format and have the following information listed:  
User that Submitted, Date and Time of Transmission, and Worksheet Status. Selecting Print Preview from the bottom menu allows for viewing the DBQ in a read-only print preview version.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/192.png)

<span id="_Toc218082574" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑170. Viewing a Signed DBQ in read-only

Users can also view the DBQ transmission history report for the selected worksheet by clicking the Transmission History button. This will launch a separate window displaying all historical information, DBQ Name, Patient, Sender, Transmission Date, and Transmission Status for the selected DBQ (Figure 2‑171)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/193.png)

<span id="_Ref160438695" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑171 Signed DBQ Transmission History

#### Completing a Non-C&P Exam

The VHA Internal DBQ Referral flag on the top of each form controls whether to treat an exam request as a C&P exam or a "NON" C&P exam (for instance, if a Veteran takes a DBQ to their Primary Care Provider without a C&P exam request on file).

The question "Is this report a C&P evaluation in response to a request (2507) submitted by the VBA?" has been removed from the "Signature Validation" screen (Figure 2‑172Figure 2‑172) . The system will default the answer to this question to "No" if the "VHA Internal DBQ Referral" flag is "Yes" and the user will be required to check the box for "No appointment was necessary (i.e.: opinion only report)."

CAPRI will not filter the Note Title list (i.e. will display all titles), hide the section allowing the user to tie the exam back to an AMIE 2507 request, thus only creating the progress note for storage in the TIU Documents file. The system will default the answer to this question to "Yes" if the "VHA Internal DBQ Referral" flag is "No" and CAPRI will maintain its current functionality.

Once an appointment or no appointment was necessary is chosen, the following will be displayed and the examiner will choose a "Title," enter his/her "Electronic Sig. Code," and click "OK."

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/194.png)

<span id="_Ref203487971" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑172 Signature Validation—Final Signature Selection.

## CPWM Worksheet Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The functionalities detailed in this section are only available when a CPWM worksheet is open for editing. After a worksheet is opened, there are several tabs and options available. By selecting the visible buttons, the user can open other parts of the worksheet.

### Spell Check

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

A spell check feature is also available from the shortcut menu (Figure 2‑173). The spell check feature searches the current text area for misspelled words and returns a list of suggested corrections. If there are no errors, a message displays "No errors found."

The spell checker allows the user to select from a list of suggested changes or make a change using the Change to edit box. The user may also add a word to a custom dictionary by selecting Add to Dictionary or Reset Dictionary to return the dictionary to its original state.

> **NOTE:** Only the text area currently being edited is checked, not the entire template.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/195.png)

<span id="_Ref203467940" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑173 Spell Check Screen

### Options Button Functionality Details

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When Options is selected, an additional template management functionality is displayed (Figure 2‑174).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/196.png)

<span id="_Ref203468143" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑174 Options Selection Template.

#### Manage Templates

CAPRI users can add additional templates to a template that is already in use or delete a template from a group of merged templates by selecting Manage Templates on the Options menu. When a user selects ManageTemplates, a dialog box is displayed (Figure 2‑175) for the user to select templates to add to or delete from the worksheet. There must always be at least one template remaining in the ManageTemplates dialog box. An entire list of templates from a set or group of merged templates cannot be deleted without having one of the original templates remaining on the list.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/197.png)

<span id="_Ref413322680" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑175. Manage Templates Screen.

#### Restore Previous Version

When a user is actively editing a template for a selected patient, the user can select Options and Restore Previous Version to view a history of the saved entries for the current template. A dialog box showing the history of each time a template was saved is displayed (Figure 2‑176). The user can select the date/time of the save. CAPRI displays the name or names of the forms saved, date, time, the name of the person who performed the save, and the names of the templates that were saved at the selected date and time. The user can then select Load Selected Version. CAPRI then loads the templates and all data saved to the templates at the date and time in history.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/198.png)

<span id="_Ref203468203" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑176. Restore Previous Version Template Screen.

To restore previous versions of DBQ worksheet exams, select Restore Previous Version from the DBQ Options menu. The Load Worksheet from previous version window will display (4). The top panel displays the history of all previously saved exams related to the opened worksheet. The user can add previous saved exams by double clicking on the exam or selecting the exam and clicking on Add this Exam version. When finished select Load Selected Version to load the previous saved version. Select Close on the DBQ. The C&P Worksheets tab will refresh with the updated DBQ form.

Users will receive the following message (Figure 2‑177) when attempting to restore a worksheet where the worksheet has no previous saved versions.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/199.png)

<span id="_Ref172198895" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑177 No Previous versions are found for this worksheet error message.

When attempting to add a selected exam twice, the user will receive a Duplicate Exam message (Figure 2‑178).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/200.png)

<span id="_Ref172198894" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑178 Duplicate Exam message.

When selecting a previous saved version that is not the most current saved version in the list, the user will be presented with the following message to check the Exam for accuracy (Figure 2‑179).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/201.png)

<span id="_Ref171067635" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑179 Please check the Exams for accuracy message.

#### Review Events Comments

If a template has had review comments added, users can view the reviewer's comments at any time while a template is open for editing by selecting Review Events on the Options menu. After the Review Events window is displayed (Figure 2‑180), users can drag the Review Events window to the side for viewing while continuing to edit the open CPWM Template. The comments for a given date and time can be viewed by selecting the date and time from the drop down list.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/202.png)

<span id="_Ref203468327" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑180 Review Events Screen.

### Saving Template Data

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

#### Autosave

The data is automatically saved by the system every fifteen minutes, but it is good practice for the user to manually save work often (Figure 2‑182).

#### Manual Saves

The user clicks Sa<u>v</u>e to manually save the data. When the manual save box is displayed, the user can enter a customized description by typing a custom name in the box where manual save appears. Forms do not have to be completed in a single session. If the user closes a form without signing, CAPRI retains all responses and save it for completion later.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/203.png)

<span id="_Ref413322552" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑181. Manual Save Template View.

#### Saving After Selecting Close

When the user closes the worksheet by selecting Close, the user is prompted to confirm this action by choosing <u>OK</u> or <u>Cancel</u> to keep working (Figure 2‑182). If OK is selected, CAPRI saves the information currently entered on the Template. If Cancel is selected, CAPRI returns the user to the Template and a save point is not created in the save history.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/204.png)

<span id="_Ref413322444" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑182. Save Worksheet Prompt.

### Reviewer Security Key Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Users who have been assigned the Reviewer Security Key have reviewer-specific buttons inside a CPWM template (Figure 2‑183).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/205.png)

<span id="_Ref406770877" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑183. Reviewer Security Privileges View.

Selecting the Send for Signature button allows the reviewer to select a signer for the document. The document is sent to the person who is authorized to complete the upload process.

If there is a problem with the document, the reviewer can communicate with the original author by selecting Review Events to display a dialog box (Figure 2‑183).

The reviewer can browse previous activity on the document by selecting entries under the Date/Time field, if there are any. To create a new review event, the reviewer selects Add New Send Back Event. The top three fields are set automatically. The reviewer places any communication to the author in the Sent Back Comments field. When the user selects Send Back Now to complete the transaction, the status of the document is changed to Sent Back and the original author receives a notice the next time he or she logs into CAPRI. The reviewer's comments are the first thing seen when working with the sent back document.

#### Additional Considerations

A reviewer can display any document in CPWM, even if it is unsigned. Users who do not have the DVBAB CPWM REVIEWER key can only view signed documents or unsigned documents that belong to them.

#### Sending a CPWM Exam for Review

Users have the option to include comments with an exam when it is sent for review. When a user selects the Send for Review button in an open template, the user is prompted with an additional option of Yes, with a comment to the reviewer (Figure 2‑184).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/206.png)

<span id="_Ref406770830" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑184. Send CPWM for Review.

Choosing the option Yes, with a comment to the reviewer opens the Send Reviewer A Message box (Figure 2‑185). The user enters comments and then clicks <u>S</u>end to Reviewer Now.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/207.png)

<span id="_Ref406770815" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑185. Send Reviewer a Message View,

Status of the exam then changes to Review Pending and marked as New notated with a yellow N (Figure 2‑186).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/208.png)

<span id="_Ref406770806" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑186. C&P Worksheet—Review Pending View.

#### Sending a CPWM Exam for Cosignature

As previously described, CAPRI respects the CPRS Authorization/Subscription Utility (ASU) and TIU business rules for cosignature. If a user is set up to require a note signature, the signature screen appears as in (Figure 2‑187) after a title has been selected. Note that CAPRI has been modified to prevent displaying appointments with dates in the future from appearing in the list of Appointments.

If a cosignature is required and the cosigner box is not active for a particular user, IRM or the site's Clinical Application Coordinator should be contacted for assistance.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/209.png)

<span id="_Ref406770792" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑187. Signature Validation View—Expected Cosigner Selection.

Selecting the box under EXPECTED COSIGNER allows the user to select or change a previously selected cosigner. A Confirm Co-Signer dialog box is displayed in (Figure 2‑188).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/210.png)

<span id="_Ref406770779" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑188. Confirm Cosigner Prompt.

The user should verify that the correct cosigner is being selected. The cosigner should sign the document directly in CAPRI to prevent use of the co-signature transfer utility.

#### Sending a CPWM Exam for Signature

Selecting Send for Signature allows the reviewer to select a signer for the document. The document is sent to the person who is authorized to complete the upload process.

### Submitting Multiple Medical Opinions per Single 2507

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

A C&P exam request (2507) can only include a single DBQ Medical Opinion. There are two scenarios where multiple medical opinions can be submitted per a single 2507:

1.  With the PDF enhancement, DBQ Medical Opinion still operates the same, with the exception that users can now submit multiple medical opinions per single 2507 using the new button "Submit Medical Opinion" available on DBQ Medical Opinion.
    1.  This functionality takes the information filled in for the Veteran from boxes 2-9 and pastes it into Section 10 - Remarks box once "Submit Medical Opinion" button is clicked. The user can complete as many Medical Opinions as needed by performing the above steps. Each Medical Opinion is separated by lines of asterisks for clarity.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/211.png)

<span id="_Ref202885274" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑189- Submitting Multiple Medical Opinions per Single 2507

2.  For completing multiple exams with multiple medical opinions for a single 2507, conducted by multiple examiners:

> Process (with examples):

1.  The first examiner reads the 2507 and finds that there are 3 exams: DBQ Medical Opinion, DBQ Hypertension, and DBQ Derm scars.
2.  The first examiner will create a worksheet (merged template) that will include DBQ Hypertension and DBQ Medical Opinion because they are the provider to evaluate the hypertension condition and provide a medical opinion as requested in the 2507.
3.  The first examiner completes both the DBQ Hypertension and DBQ Medical Opinion then signs and submits the worksheet, which is sent to VBMS.
    1.  Note: When they sign the worksheet, they select the corresponding open exam(s).
4.  When the second examiner accesses the 2507, they will see only the remaining open DBQ Derm Scars exam and a completed DBQ Medical Opinion exam.
5.  The second examiner will create a worksheet (merged template) that will include DBQ Derm Scars and DBQ Medical Opinion because they are the provider to evaluate the derm scars condition and provide a medical opinion as requested in the 2507.
6.  The second examiner completes both the DBQ Derm scars and DBQ Medical Opinion then signs and submits the worksheet, which is sent to VBMS.
    1.  Note: When they sign the worksheet, they select the corresponding open exam(s).

> **NOTE:** The process will be repeated for 2507s that required any number of exams with separate examiners who all needed to complete separate DBQ medical opinions.

## Vocational Rehabilitation Tab

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Overview

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Vocational Rehabilitation and Employment (VR&E) employees require the ability to refer a Veteran to a medical center for the purpose of specific types of exams or opinions in order to determine VR&E benefits.

This feature enables users to create an electronic request for medical services, track those requests and print reports. A consult is created outside the CAPRI application. The request can be linked to the consult by a VHA Coordinator. Notifications are sent to both VR&E Counselor (via Outlook) and VHA Coordinator (via MAILMAN) when the 28-8861 form status is updated. A nightly background job monitors consults that are linked to the 28-8861 file.

### User Setup

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

#### Security Key Assignment

To activate the VocRehab tab for a particular user, depending on their role, the following security keys need to be given:

DVBA CAPRI WORKSHEET

DVBAB CPWM REQUIRE REVIEW

DVBAB CPWM DISALLOW REVIEW

DVBAB CPWM OPTIONAL REVIEW

DVBAB CPWM REVIEWER

DVBA CAPRI VRE_COUNSELOR – This security key is given to the VBA/VR&E on the CLAIMS system so that the VocRehab tab is available to the user. This key must also be given to the VHA Coordinator role on the local VistAsystem for the VocRehab tab to be available to the user.

DVBA CAPRI VHA_COORDINATOR – The VHA Coordinator role needs this security key assigned on the local VistA system to allow the ability to assign a consult to a Medical Services Request. The <u>A</u>ssign Consults button located on the Medical Services Requests screen is to be only available to the VHA Coordinator role.

### VocRehab Tab Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The VocRehab tab provides access to the Medical Services Requests screen (Figure 2‑201). The user must have the proper security key (DVBA CAPRI VRE COUNSELOR) for this tab to be visible.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/223.png)

<span id="_Ref514864961" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑201. VocRehab Tab View.

#### Add a New Request (VBA/VR&E Counselor Role)

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the VocRehab tab (Figure 2‑201).

Step 2 – The user clicks on the Add <u>N</u>ew Request button to add a new Chapter 31, VA Form 28-8861 request (Medical Services Request).

The field explanations on the screenshot shown in (Figure 2‑202) are listed below:

- Patient Name: This is the name of the patient selected from the Patient Selector screen. This is a read-only field and is not editable.
- VA Claim#: If a VA Claim Number exists in the New Patient file for the selected Veteran, it is displayed here. This is a read-only field and is not editable.
- Request Date: This is the date the request is initiated. This is set by the system clock and is not a user editable field.
- Anticipated Date of Rehabilitation: This is a required field noting a future date when rehabilitation is anticipated to be complete.
- Regional Office: This is the requestor's Regional Office and is derived from the user's log in.
- Point of Contact: This button displays a screen with the Point of Contact information. This is the user who entered the Medical Services Request
- Medical Services Requested: Users must choose from a predefined list of medical services. If the medical service resides outside of this list, Other can be selected. The Other Description field is then enabled.
- Other Description: This field is enabled if Other is chosen in the Medical Services Requested field. This is a required field if enabled.
- Reason for Referral: This is a required field. It is a free text field that is enabled for a maximum of 50 characters to briefly describe the reason a referral is being made.
- Rehabilitation Objectives: This is a required field. It is a free text field that is enabled for a maximum of 100 characters to allow input of rehabilitation objectives.
- Comments / Notes: This is a free text field allowing for brief or long descriptions relating to this medical request. The field is always editable unless the Request for Medical Service has been cancelled or completed.
- Preferred Schedule Date: If the Veteran needs to be seen by a certain date, a Preferred Schedule Date can be entered.
- Preferred Schedule Date Reason: This field cannot be blank if a Preferred Schedule Date is specified. One needs to explain the reason for specifying a Preferred Schedule Date.
- <u>S</u>end Medical Services Request button. This button submits all the data input and initiates the Medical Services Request
- <u>C</u>ancel button: This button closes the form without saving a "new" Medical Services Request.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/224.png)

<span id="_Ref406770521" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑202. VocRehab Add New Medical Services Request.

#### Edit Request (VBA/VR&E Counselor and VHA Coordinator Roles)

Step 1 – The user logs into CAPRI, looks up the Veteran using the Patient Selector, and moves to the VocRehab tab.

Step 2 – The user clicks on the date of the request they would like to edit and clicks Edit Request button to edit a Chapter 31, VA Form 28-8861 request (Medical Services Request).

The fields for editing a Medical Services Request and adding one are identical and have the following exceptions:

- Consult Selected: This field is read-only. It displays "No Consults Found" if a consult has NOT been assigned to the Medical Services Request yet or it displays the Date, Time, and Consult Name of the consult assigned to this Medical Services Request
- Assign Consult button: This button opens the Assign Consult dialog box and allows the user to associate a consult with a request. It is only selectable if the user has the security key of DVBA CAPRI VHA_COORDINATOR (VHA Coordinator role)
- Update Medical Services Request button. This updates the request.
- Cancel Medical Services Request button: This button cancels the Medical Services Request. The request remains in the database for historical purposes, but its status shows as Cancelled. This button is not available on a new request, or if a consult has already been assigned.
- Once a consult has been assigned to this Medical Services Request, the only field that can be edited is the Comments / Notes field. All other fields are read-only.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/225.png)

<span id="_Toc218082607" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑203 VocRehab Edit Medical Services Request.

#### Point of Contact

This screen (Figure 2‑204) displays the Point of Contact information for the Medical Services Request. It is derived from the user's credentials that logged into CAPRI and entered the request.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/226.png)

<span id="_Ref406770389" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑204. VocRehab Medical Services Point of Contact View.

#### Assign Consults

This screen (Figure 2‑205) is only visible to users who have a security key of DVBA CAPRI VHA_COORDINATOR. The user selects a consult that was previously created for the Veteran in CPRS to assign to the Medical Services Request under the VocRehab tab. This assignment must take place in order for the consult status to be tracked. Only one consult can be assigned to one Medical Services Request.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/227.png)

<span id="_Ref406770374" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑205. VocRehab Medical Services Assign Consults View.

#### Cancel Request

The cancel request screen (Figure 2‑206), allows a user to cancel a Medical Services Request. This option is only available if a consult has not already been linked (assigned) to this request. A request cancellation reason must be selected from the choices displayed on the screen. If Other is selected as a reason, an explanation must be entered in the Cancellation Explanation field.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/228.png)

<span id="_Ref406770357" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑206. VocRehab Medical Services Cancellation Request.

#### Notification Messages

Upon specific events, MAILMAN messages are sent to VHA Coordinators who must be a member of the MAILMAN mail group: DVBA VR VOCREHAB PERSONNEL and the VR&E Counselor(s) receive email notifications via Outlook. These notifications are sent for the following status changes to the Medical Services Request:

- New Status: This message reads: Chapter 31 Referral for Medical Services New. \*\*This notification is only sent to the VHA Coordinator via MAILMAN
- Pending Status: This message reads: Chapter 31 Referral for Medical Services Pending. \*\*In CAPRI, once the VHA Coordinator/or designated person links the consult request to the original Chapter 31, VA Form 28-8861 request, the status of the consult in CAPRI is then changed to Pending. Both the VHA Coordinator and VR&E Counselor receive notification messages
- Completed Status: This message reads: Chapter 31 Referral for Medical Services Completed \*\* Both the VHA Coordinator and VR&E Counselor receive notification messages.
- Cancelled Status: This message reads: Chapter 31 Referral for Medical Services Cancelled\*\* Both the VHA Coordinator and VR&E Counselor receive notification messages.

(Figure 2‑207), is an example of a New Status MAILMAN message the VHA Coordinator would receive.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/229.png)

<span id="_Ref406770338" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑207. VocRehab Notification Message.

#### Aging Notification Messages

Aging notification messages (Figure 2‑208), are sent when a New Medical Services Request age five, six, and seven days. Five- and six-day notifications are sent alerting the VHA Coordinator via Mailman to link a consult to the Medical Services Request. Seven-day aging notifications are sent to the VR&E Counselor via Outlook alerting them that no consult has been linked to the form yet.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/230.png)

<span id="_Ref406770321" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑208. VocRehab Aged Notification Message.

## Additional CAPRI Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### CAPRI Remote Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

CAPRI Remote users access CAPRI through the Claims system, which runs on the VHA Forum hardware. The server for those users should be set to CLAIMS server and the port number.

CAPRI Remote users outside of VBA normally obtain an access code from the Office of Information (OI) support staff, not from the local field site. Most VBA users are CAPRI Remote users. Most VHA users are local site users. If a user starts CAPRI and does not successfully connect to a VistA system within 90 seconds, CAPRI automatically shuts down.

When users log into CAPRI remotely, CAPRI alerts users when no email account is set up.

### VistA Terminal

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The "Attachmate Reflections Secure Shell" application replaces the telnet window. CAPRI Remote users can launch a VistA Terminal session by selecting the VistA button to log into the local VistA system site they are assigned to. A dialog box is displayed when the VistA button is clicked that provides the user the ability to choose between connecting using the secure shell application or telnet. The default is set to secure shell application.

> **NOTE:** Local CAPRI users do not have access to the VistA Terminal from CAPRI and the VistA button is not available.

### Switch Sites – On the File Menu

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The Switch Sites option, available on the File Menu (Figure 2‑209), is active if the user is accessing a VistA system with remote capability. If the selection is active, the user may have other VistA accounts they can connect to without re-authenticating.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/231.png)

<span id="_Ref406770303" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑209. CAPRI File Menu—Switch Sites Selection.

CAPRI Remote users can use this option to switch from one site to another without logging out of CAPRI. This option is only available when users are logged into the claims server with remote access. Users can access the Switch Sites… option from the File menu. After Switch Sites… is selected, the user is prompted with the site list to allow them to choose the site to which they would like to switch.

### Check Remote Connections

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Check Remote Connections, found on the Help menu, is available to users with @ or U level FileMan access (Figure 2‑210). This provides a utility to scan CAPRI remote sites to discover any connectivity issues.

To use this function, the user selects the connections to scan in the left list box or clicks SelectAll and then Run Selected. Any connection problems are listed in the Could not connect to field. This does not mean that the site is down nationwide; it means that the current user cannot access the site, which could be due to several reasons.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/232.png)

<span id="_Ref406770290" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑210. Validate Remote Connections View.

### Send to VBA eFolder– On the File Menu

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

This feature creates an interface between CAPRI and the Claims Evidence Webservice. It allows CAPRI users to electronically transmit CAPRI-generated Veteran-related documents that *<u>can be printed today</u>* to the VBA eFolder. The Send to VBA eFolder option (Figure 2‑211) is available within the File menu and from the Send to VBA eFolder button within the <u>Clinical Documents Report Builder</u> application. In order to send clinical documents to VBA eFolder users must hold the DVBA CAPRI CLIN DOC-EFOLDER security key and fall under one of the following user categories:

- VBA – All VBA Users should have access to upload clinical documents to the VBMS eFolder.
- VHA – Limited, only specific roles should have access to upload clinical documents to the VBMS eFolder.
- Spokane – All providers at the Spokane site should have access to upload clinical documents to the VBMS eFolder.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/233.png)

<span id="_Ref71017913" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑211. CAPRI File Menu – Send to VBA eFolder Selection.

Documents within the following tabs of CAPRI have the capability of being transmitted to the VBA eFolder via the File\>Send to VBA eFolder (Figure 2‑211).

- Reports tab (patient related reports only)
- Admin tab
- Health Summaries tab
- Clinical Documents tab
- [Report Builder](#report-builder) (see Section 2.8.2)

#### Sending Documentation to VBA eFolder

Functionality from within the Clinical Documents tab allows the user to use File-\>Send to VBA eFolder menu option or the user can choose Send to VBA eFolder from the Report Builder window after the Build button has been selected.

1.  Once Send to VBA eFolder has been selected, the user is prompted with a confirmation message of, "Are you sure you want to send document(s) to the VBA record of Veteran Name?" (Figure 2‑212)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/234.png)

<span id="_Ref406770251" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑212. Transmit to VBA eFolder Prompt.

2.  After selecting Yes, to transmit, CAPRI will automatically validate the user's PIV certificate.
3.  After selecting OK on the CAPRI Certificate Selection window (Figure 2‑213), the user will be prompted to enter their Personal Identity Verification (PIV) pin, select OK to continue transmission (Figure 2‑214).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/235.png)

<span id="_Ref110000906" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑213. CAPRI Certificate Selection Window

PIV PIN Authentication: When presented, enter PIV PIN to authenticate and select OK to transmit. If Cancel is selected, the user will receive a "Transmission cancelled by user." dialog message. (Figure 2‑226)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/236.png)

<span id="_Ref105147928" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑214 PIV Authentication Screen

The following dialog box (Figure 2‑215) is displayed about the successful status of transmission.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/237.png)

<span id="_Ref406770238" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑215. VBA eFolder Transmission

#### Send to VBA eFolder Transmission Error Codes

If the transmission fails, an error message will display with an error code describing the failure and the status of the transmitting file to VBA eFolder

Error code: 400 Bad Request. This error indicates the server was unable to understand the attempted transmission.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/238.png)

<span id="_Toc218082620" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑216 Transmission Error Code: 400 Bad Request

Error code: 400 MTLS error. This error indicates that the user is not authorized to access the resource.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/239.png)

<span id="_Toc218082621" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑217 Transmission Error Code: 400 MTLS

Error code: 401 Unauthorized User. This error indicates the server did not receive a properly formed authorization token (JWT).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/240.png)

<span id="_Toc218082622" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑218 Transmission Error Code: 401 Unauthorized User

Error code: 403 File number error. This error indicates the Veteran file number did not resolve.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/241.png)

<span id="_Toc218082623" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑219 Transmission Error Code: 403 File Number

Error code: 500 Series Server Error. This error indicates the transmission encountered an issue when processing the request.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/242.png)

<span id="_Toc218082624" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑220 Transmission Error code: 500 Series Server

Error code: 900 Transmission Disabled. This error message indicates that transmissions to eFolder are disabled for this site.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/243.png)

<span id="_Ref110081942" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2-‑221 Transmission Error code: 900 Transmission Disabled

Clinical Document Transmission Error – proxy transmission. When a proxy transmission encounters a connection timeout, the tranmssion will retry up to five times. When failing after the fifth attempt, the transmission will result in error code 901 (Figure 2‑223).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/244.png)

<span id="_Toc218082626" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑222. Proxy Transmission Timed Out After 5 Attempts

Error code: 901 Connection Timeout. This error occurs when the transmission times out after five transmission attempts. The user should try to transmit again.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/245.png)

<span id="_Ref110081992" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑223 Transmission Error code: 901 Timed Out

Clinical Document Transmission Error – Proxy TLS. When a proxy transmission encounters a TLS error, the tranmssion will retry up to five times. When failing after the fifth attempt, the transmission will result in error code 75788.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/246.png)

<span id="_Toc218082628" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑224. Proxy TLS Transmission Retry

Error code 75788: Error message that is displayed if the error code is anything other than 400,401 or 500. This will be displayed if an unknown error code is received, typically after the Proxy TLS tranmission has timed out after 5 attempts.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/247.png)

<span id="_Ref110082030" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑225 Transmission Default Error Code 75788

Transmission cancelled by user dialog message if cancelling during PIV authentication (Figure 2‑226 Transmission Cancelled by User Dialog).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/248.png)

<span id="_Ref105147688" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑226 Transmission Cancelled by User Dialog

Error code 96260: Socket error. Please contact local IT or enter a service ticket.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/249.png)

<span id="_Ref110082959" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑227. Socket Error

The maximum size of a document(s) that can be transmitted is 15mb. If the document(s) that have been selected exceed this size the user is notified with the following message in.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/250.png)

<span id="_Ref406770178" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑228. VBA eFolder Transmission File Size Error Prompt.

When users attempt to transmit a patient file to eFolder that is missing a claim of file number, the transmission will fail, and the error message below will be displayed. Users will need to manually upload the documents to eFolder (2.13.6 Manually Upload Documents to eFolder).

Error message: "CAPRI cannot transmit your documents to the VBA eFolder automatically because a claim number or alternate linking data cannot be located in VistA for this patient. As a result, you must upload the document(s) to the correct VBA eFolder manually by converting them into a PDF format and then uploading them via VBMS. See the CAPRI User Guide for more information."

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/251.png)

<span id="_Ref121826673" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑229 CAPRI Cannot Transmit Your Document Claim Number or Data not Located Error

### Manually Upload Documents to eFolder

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When the automatic transmission of clinical documents from CAPRI to VBMS eFolder is unavailable, users will need to manually upload the documents. For example, if a user's PIV authentication fails or a Veteran patient cannot be identified, the user will need to manually upload PDF versions of the clinical docs to the correct eFolder in VBMS

1.  Select the Clinical Document to upload to eFolder. You can use Report Builder to select multiple documents that can be built into one (see section 2.8.2).![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/252.png)
2.  From the CAPRI menu, select File.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/253.png)
3.  Select Print.
4.  In the Print window under Name, select Microsoft Print to PDF.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/254.png)
5.  Press OK.
6.  Enter the file name and select Save.
7.  The CAPRI Dialog window will appear with the message, "This report has been sent to the printer." select OK.
8.  Locate and open the PDF file.
9.  Manually upload the PDF versions of the clinical document(s) to the corresponding VBMS eFolder. (For additional information on eFolder, please refer to the VBMS User Manual).

### Get Docs from DAS - On the File Menu

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

FILE\|GET DOCS FROM DAS has been created to allow CAPRI VHA users to retrieve and view documents from central data store DAS. The user MUST hold the DVBA CAPRI GETDOCSFROMVLER security key to access this functionality.

To access the GET DOCS FROM DAS:

> **NOTE:** All queries must have a Date Range and at least one other search criteria.

The "Retrieve Exam Documents from DAS" window will be displayed with results from search criteria selected and allow the CAPRI user to open documents for the selected Veteran, by clicking on document and choosing "View Selected Document" button or by double clicking on the document. This window allows the user to sort by column and view the sort in Ascending or Descending order.

### Validate the Patient Restricted List

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

A CAPRI Remote user can have restricted access to patient records they are authorized to view.

> **NOTE:** As determined by the Health Information Access (HIA) Office, the CAPRI application shuts down if the CAPRI remote user is unable to access their restricted patient list on the CLAIMS server. Prior to the CAPRI application being shut down, the CAPRI Remote user receives a notification that their restricted patient list could not be accessed, and they are provided HIA contact information. The CAPRI Remote user also sees the restricted patient list that could not be accessed displayed in the lower left status bar of the CAPRI mail form.

#### Performing HIA Search

While in the CAPRI application, navigate to the Tools drop down menu (Figure 2‑237). Select the option HIA Search. Click the <u>S</u>elect File button to have the Open dialog window display.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/262.png)

<span id="_Ref406770123" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑237. CAPRI Tolls Menu—HIS Search Selection.

Note 1: There are two methods that create the same results. The preferred method is to click the Select File button. The other method is by clicking on the main menu Process (Upper left) and then selecting the Import (Figure 2‑238) option.

Note 2: The legend at the bottom of this screen reflects the possible MVI search results.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/263.png)

<span id="_Ref406770105" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑238. HIA Search View.

In the Open dialog window (Figure 2‑239) select the Patient Restricted list file, .csv file type.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/264.png)

<span id="_Ref406770082" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑239. HIA Search—File Selection.

Ensure that the correct selected file name appears in the HIA Selected File: window displayed in (Figure 2‑240). If the HIA Selected File name is NOT correct, click on the <u>S</u>elect File button to choose a different file name. If correct click the <u>V</u>erify button near the bottom of the screen. Click the <u>C</u>ancel button (next to the Verify button) to return to the CAPRI home screen.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/265.png)

<span id="_Ref406770069" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑240. HIA Search—Cancel to Close.

<span id="_Ref406770056" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑241. HIA Search Results within the MVI database.

#### Print Option Method

Select the File menu option, then select the Print option shown in the following (Figure 2‑242).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/267.png)

<span id="_Ref406770036" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑242. HIA Search File Menu—Print Selection.

#### Export Method Option

Select the Process menu option, then select the HIA Export Result option shown in the following figure:

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/270.png)

<span id="_Ref406770005" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑245. HIA Search Process Menu—HIA Export Result.

The Save As dialog box displays, (Figure 2‑246). Select your Save in location to store your .csv file. Next accept the default File Name: field or create a file name of your choice ensuring the file extension remains .csv. The Save as type: option is set only for .csv file format. Click the Save button to complete the file save option.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/271.png)

<span id="_Ref406769993" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑246. HIA Search Export File Save.

To view the result of your .csv file, navigate to the location that you saved the file. By default, if you have the MS Excel application installed, you can double click on the file name and the file opens in an Excel format shown (Figure 2‑247). If changes are made to your open Excel file and it is desired to save them and keep the file in the .csv file type, select File from the main menu, then select the Save As option. The Save As dialog box displays, then select the Save as type drop down list. Select option: CSV(MS-DOS) (\*.csv) and click on the Save button to complete. You can also save your new file in an Excel file format by following the same method but save it as a standard Excel format.

You can also right-click on the file name and select open with the Notepad application. Notepad displays the results in a common delimited format. Once the file is opened in Notepad and if changes are made that you desire to retain, select File and click Save and it saves at the existing location as a .csv file type.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/272.png)

<span id="_Ref406769978" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑247. HIA Search File Save Results.

### Edit Remote User Site Access

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The Remote User Sites Editor window, found on the Help menu, is available to users with @ or U level FileMan access (Figure 2‑248). This tool allows a CAPRI administrator to change the remote sites for specific CAPRI users, either limiting their site list or providing the full list of VHA VistA systems to the user. This list is presented to the administrator when CAPRI is started in remote mode.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/273.png)

<span id="_Ref406769822" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑248. Remote User Sites Editor.

The User: list is generated from the Claims system, which runs on the Forum hardware hosted by the VHA Office of Information in Silver Spring, MD. The DVBAB RESTRICTED LIST PATIENTS remote procedure is used to restrict unauthorized patient record access.

To begin the process, the administrator selects a name from the User: column. The second column is updated with the user's site access privileges. Selecting Yes for Show all sites? displays every available VistA site on the user's Authorized Sites: list. If the user should have access to only a limited number of sites, then the administrator should select No for this radio group and update the user's allowed sites under Authorized Sites.

One user's site list can be copied to other users. This is useful when a known group of users accesses the same sites. This function is accomplished using the third column of the utility.

- Step 1- A user is selected and the list of sites in the second column is updated.

  Step 2- After the user is set up correctly, the Use Selected as Parent Clone button is selected. This user is then listed in the Parent Clone edit box.

  Step 3- Each user who should have the same site list is selected, and the Add Selected to Child Clone List button is clicked.

  Step 4- After the list is set up correctly, the process is completed by selecting the Start Cloning button.

Audit Trail download utility, found on the Help menu, is available to users with @ or U level FileMan access and who have been provided an additional secondary menu option on the Claims system (Figure 2‑250). This tool is used by VBA to download CAPRI audit data for internal reporting. Although a user may see this option on the menu, the secondary menu option that allows it to run may not be provided.
