## Miscellaneous CAPRI Functionality

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Edit Exam List Parameters 

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The menu option Edit Exam List Parameter (MAS) is under Tools on the CAPRI menu bar. Exam List Parameter (MAS) is a tool used by sites to define which exams are performed at a division. When an exam request is made via the C&P exams tab, the exam list displays exams as performed or not performed in a division according to this definition (Figure 2‑255).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/280.png)

<span id="_Ref203488071" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑255 CAPRI Tools Menu—Edit Exam List Parameters

The Security Key DVBA CAPRI EXAM LIST EDIT should be assigned to the MAS ADPAC, C&P Clinic Clerk, or other individual who is responsible for maintaining the list of C&P exams performed at the site.

The user, who has the Security Key above or programmer access, should see Edit Exam List Parameter (MAS) under the Tools menu option after logging into the CAPRI GUI executable.

If the user does not have option context DVBA CAPRI GUI, it should be assigned to their secondary menu.

For users who do not hold this Security Key or who have @ FileMan access, the option is not displayed. When a user clicks Edit Exam List Parameter (MAS), the dialog box in Figure 2‑256 displays. Parameters that are set in this window are displayed in the Add a New C&P Exam dialog box.

Modifications were made to the "Edit Exam List Parameters" form/option to add functionality to control the disabling of a Routing Location for a 2507 C&P Exam Request and a 7131 Request (Release of Information) (Figure 2‑256).

If a new division(s) has/have been added, the next time a user uses this option it sets the new division(s) to inactive by default for both 2507 C&P Exam Requests and 7131 Requests. CAPRI users can manually activate the new division(s) by unchecking the 'Completely Inactive this Division's checkbox.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/281.png)

<span id="_Ref79657573" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑256. Edit Exam List Parameters Screen.

To turn off a division so it is not available for user selection, the user selects the division on the left of the screen then selects one or both of the Completely Inactivate this Division… boxes for C&P Exam Requests and/or 7131 Requests in the Edit Exam List Parameters (MAS) window as shown in (Figure 2‑257). A textbox is provided for comments relating to a specific division.

When selecting Edit Exam List Parameters (MAS) for the first time, CAPRI automatically finds the site's divisions and activates all exams for each division in a default list. The user is then able to add new lists or modify the default list that was created.

To enable a specified group of users to order certain exams, the user may create a custom list by selecting the Add New button in the Edit Exam List Parameters (MAS) window.

When the user selects an exam list defined for a division, the Edit Selected button is enabled. Selecting this button triggers the display of the screen below (Figure 2‑257) to enable users to modify the list of exams and add comments about a particular list or exam.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/282.png)

<span id="_Ref406769699" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑257. Edit Exam List Parameters Dialog Window.

The checkmarks in the checkboxes next to the exam name indicate that the exams are activated.

Users can modify (turn off) the default as well as the custom exam list by clearing the check marks from the checkboxes next to the exams they wish to turn off. This causes the exam to display in red in the Add a New Exam dialog box, indicating that the exam is not performed at the particular facility.

If exams are turned off via future DVB patches, they no longer display in the New Exam dialog box.

If a patch releases a new exam, it is shown to all users until someone uses Edit Exam List Parameter (MAS) to turn it off.

Comments may be added for the exam list in the section labeled Comment for this list of exams. Users can place comments, reminders, or notes about an exam in the text box labeled, Commentto be displayed when this exam is selected. Selecting the exam brings the textbox into view.

Additionally, a functionality has been added that allows facilities to disable the ability to submit C&P Exam Request and/or 7131 Requests by disabling the "Add a New Request" button specific to each of those requests (Figure 2‑258). When selecting to disable the ability to submit either of the requests, a pop-up confirmation message will appear (Figure 2‑259 & Figure 2‑260). Click Yes to confirm the action.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/283.png)

<span id="_Ref79594213" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑258. Facility Control – Disable Request Submissions

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/284.png)

<span id="_Ref79598544" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑259. Confirm Disabling Ability to Add New C&P Exams

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/285.png)

<span id="_Ref79598562" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑260. Confirm Disabling Ability to Add New 7131 Requests

Once confirmed, the user will see a confirmation message on the respective tab that the site is no longer accepting that specific request (Figure 2‑261 & Figure 2‑262). The "Add a New Request" button will now be disabled.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/286.png)

<span id="_Ref79598767" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑261. Site Not Accepting C&P Exams Notification

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/287.png)

<span id="_Ref79598779" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑262. Site Not Accepting 7131 Requests Notification

### Joint Longitudinal Viewer (JLV) Tab

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

> **NOTE:** Training materials for the JLV system are available on the JLV Resources SharePoint site

The Joint Longitudinal Viewer (JLV) tab has replaced the VistAWeb tab as part of the GUI 193.12 update in preparation of the VistAWeb end of lifecycle. The Joint Longitudinal Viewer tab supplies a button that launches the JLV URL in an external browser while keeping patient context. The JLV provides an integrated read-only view of health data from all VA, and VA community partner sites where the Veteran or Service member has received care.

Prior to the release of DVBA\*2.7\*240, JLV launched patient context information within Internet Explorer. This functionality has been updated to launch patient context information within the user's default browser as Internet Explorer will be retired and no longer supported as of June 15, 2022.

When selecting the JLV button, the user will be presented with a CAPRI alert message, informing them that the JLV will launch and attempt to load patient information in addition to a reminder to close the JLV browser tab when the user switches to a new patient within CAPRI. (Figure 2‑264)

When switching patients within CAPRI, users will be receiving an alert prompt informing them that JLV was previously launched and to close the previous JLV browser tab to protect patient context information, to keep from accidentally entering wrong information into the previous patient into the current patient's data. (Figure 2‑265)

When selecting, Select Patient and entering the Patient ID, after selecting the patient and moving from the C&P exams tab to the Joint Longitudinal Viewer tab and selecting the JLV Joint Longitudinal Viewer button, if the patient selected does not have an ICN number assigned, you'll receive a CAPRI Dialog alert, "Unable to locate the required information to load this patient within JLV" (Figure 2‑266)

Users will receive an alert, "Could not identify JLV URL" when unable to communicate with the JLV website. As this alert is rare, it may be possible that you've lost internet connection. (Figure 2‑267)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/288.png)

<span id="_Toc218082667" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑263. Joint Longitudinal Viewer (JLV) launching button

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/289.png)

<span id="_Ref94217180" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑264 Remember to close the JLV tab when switching to a new patient

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/290.png)

<span id="_Ref94217242" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑265 JLV was previously launched, close the previous JLV browser tab to protect patient information

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/291.png)

<span id="_Ref94217293" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑266 Unable to locate patient ICN

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/292.png)

<span id="_Ref94217331" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑267 Could not identify JLV URL

> **NOTE:** In order to maintain patient context information between CAPRI and Joint Longitudinal Viewer, remember to close the JLV browser each time a patient or site is switched.

## Set Up Options

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

### Printer Set Up

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

CAPRI has Windows printer functionality. The user can generate printouts using the default printer selection.

1.  The user selects Printer Setup from the File menu (Figure 2‑268).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/293.png)

<span id="_Ref406769535" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑268. CAPRI File Menu—Printer Setup Selection.

2.  CAPRI displays the current default printer. If the user has more than one printer, selecting the down arrow at the end of the printer name displays a list of available printers. To permanently change the default printer, the user must contact IRM or use the printer's applet under the Windows control panel.

### Display Properties

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Display properties of CAPRI can be modified using Windows DPI settings.

1.  Right click on an empty part of your Desktop and select Display Settings.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/294.png)

2.  Scroll down to Scale and Layout.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/295.png)

<span id="_Toc218082673" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑269. Display Settings Page

3.  Click the dropdown and select the appropriate Scale and Layout value. This will change the size of text, apps and other items

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/296.png)

<span id="_Toc218082674" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑270. Scale and Layout

To set Accessibility, go to the Start/Control Panel/Ease of Access/Ease of Access Center (Figure 2‑271).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/297.png)

<span id="_Ref406769301" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑271. Windows Ease of Access View.

Selecting Change Font takes the user to the standard Windows Fonts dialog box that can also be opened via Start/Control Panel/ Appearance and Personalization /Fonts.

Patient Lookup Keyboard Sensitivity (Figure 2‑271) adjusts the amount of time CAPRI waits after a keystroke before searching for the patient on the Patient Selector screen. The user should not change this from the default setting of 500 milliseconds unless there are difficulties.

If the user makes any changes to these settings and wants to undo the changes, then the user should select Restore Original Font/Keyboard Settings (Figure 2‑271).

### Change Forwarding Address

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Change Forwarding Address on the Tools menu allows the user to specify an Exchange email address for forwarding MailMan messages and CAPRI reports (Figure 2‑272). The user can find an Exchange email address by opening the Address Book in the default email application and searching for the desired name. After the user finds the name and right-clicks it, Properties is selected. Then, the user should go to the Email Addresses tab. Local IRM staff should be contacted if the user has difficulty identifying the email addresses.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/298.png)

<span id="_Ref406769125" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑272. CAPRI Tools Menu—Change Forwarding Address Selection.

### Forwarding Email Address

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

This email address is used for automated AMIE responses to certain activity such as cancelled C&P exam requests and completed C&P exam request bulletins. The responses are emailed back to the user if there is an email address specified (Figure 2‑273).

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/299.png)<span id="_Toc278187876" class="anchor"></span>

Figure 2‑273. Email Forwarding Address Dialog Window.

### Help Menu

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

For most users, this menu only has two items, About (Figure 2‑275) and CAPRI C&P Template Training Website. Other items only show up for users who have been assigned these options.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/300.png)

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/301.png)

<span id="_Ref406769060" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑274. CAPRI Help Menu.

#### About

The AboutCAPRI screen displays the CAPRI version number (Figure 2‑275). The user can also see this number on the CAPRI splash screen at start-up or when switching sites when using CAPRI in remote mode.

The user can select OK (Figure 2‑275), to close the About CAPRI screen. The user should have this information available when reporting a problem.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/302.png)

<span id="_Ref406769037" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑275. About CAPRI View.

#### Training Website

Selecting CAPRI C&P Template Training Website takes the user to the CAPRI website with video training materials.

#### Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons

Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons allows a user to add/inactivate/reactivate 2507 Cancellation Reasons. This option is available from the HELP menu.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/303.png)

<span id="_Toc218082680" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑276. CAPRI Help Menu—Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons Selection.

This option will only be available to CAPRI-Remote (national) users who are assigned security key "DVBA CAPRI EXAMCANC."

<u>SECURITY KEY ALLOCATION</u>:DVBA CAPRI EXAMCANC – must be assigned to a user that will need access to the "Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons" option.

<u>MANAGE EXAM CANCELLATION REASONS</u> option functionality:

1.  From the HELP menu choose "Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons" option
2.  The "Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons" window will be displayed

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/304.png)

<span id="_Toc218082681" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑277. Manage Exam Cancellation Screen.

3.  To Inactivate an Active reason, choose the reason in the Active list and click the "Inactivate" button. This will move the active reason chosen to the Inactive list column.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/305.png)

<span id="_Toc218082682" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑278. Exam Cancellation Screen—Active Selection.

4.  To Activate an Inactive reason, choose the reason from the Inactive list and click the "Activate" button. This will move the inactive reason chosen to the Active list column.

> ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/306.png)

<span id="_Toc218082683" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑279. Exam Cancellation Screen—Inactive Selection

5.  To add a cancellation reason, click the "Add New Cancellation Reason" button

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/307.png)

<span id="_Toc218082684" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑280. Exam Cancellation Screen—Add New Cancellation Reason.

6.  Enter the new cancellation reason in the text field. Cancellation reason must be 3-40 characters in length.

> ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/308.png)

<span id="_Toc218082685" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑281. Exam Cancellation Screen—Cancellation Reason.

7.  To save the cancellation reason just entered, click "Save Cancellation Reason" button. Once saved, the reason entered will be automatically added to the "Active" list column.
8.  If you do not want to save the cancellation reason, click "Cancel" button. Reason will "not" be added to either the Active or Inactive list columns.
9.  To EXIT the "Manage Exam Cancellation Reasons" window click the "Close" button or "X" to close the window.

#### Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons

Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons allows a user to add/inactivate/reactivate 2507 Insufficient Reasons. This option is available from the HELP menu.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/309.png)

<span id="_Toc218082686" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑282. CAPRI Help Menu—Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons Selection

This option will only be available to CAPRI-Remote (national) users who are assigned security key "DVBA CAPRI EXAMCANC."

<u>SECURITY KEY ALLOCATION</u>:DVBA CAPRI EXAMINSUFF – must be assigned to a user that will need access to the "Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons" option.

<u>MANAGE EXAM INSUFFICIENT REASONS</u> option functionality:

1.  From the HELP menu choose "Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons" option
2.  The "Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons" window will be displayed

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/310.png)

<span id="_Toc218082687" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑283. Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons.

3.  To Inactivate an Active reason, choose the reason in the Active list and click the "Inactivate" button. This will move the active reason chosen to the Inactive list column.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/311.png)

<span id="_Toc218082688" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑284. Exam Insufficient Reasons—Active Selection.

4.  To Activate an Inactive reason, choose the reason from the Inactive list and click the "Activate" button. This will move the inactive reason chosen to the Active list column.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/312.png)

<span id="_Toc218082689" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑285. Exam Insufficient Reasons—Inactive Selection

5.  To add an insufficient reason, click the "Add New Insufficient Reason" button

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/313.png)

<span id="_Toc218082690" class="anchor"></span>Figure 2‑286. Exam Insufficient Reasons—Add New Insufficient Reason.

    ![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/314.png)

7.  To save the insufficient reason just entered, click "Save Insufficient Reason" button. Once saved, the reason entered will be automatically added to the "Active" list column.
8.  If you do not want to save the insufficient reason, click "Cancel" button. Reason will "not" be added to either the Active or Inactive list columns.
9.  To EXIT the "Manage Exam Insufficient Reasons" window click the "Close" button or "X" to close the window.

# Troubleshooting and Error Messages

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

> **NOTE:** In all cases, please print the error message for your local IRM staff.

## CAPRI Not Installed in VistA

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑1), if the VHA Medical Center has not loaded the VHA half of the CAPRI software. The user should contact local IRM staff after receiving this message.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/316.png)

<span id="_Ref514864854" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑1. CAPRI Not Loaded on Server Alert.

## CAPRI GUI Option Not Assigned to User in VistA

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑2), if the VHA medical center has not assigned the CAPRI option to RO users. The user must contact local IRM staff upon receiving this message.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/317.png)

<span id="_Ref514864836" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑2. CAPRI User Access Alert.

## VistA Server Down

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑3), or a similar one such as WSAETIMEDOUT, when there are performance issues in the VA Wide Area Network (WAN), if a server is down, or if a server was not restarted after being down. When this happens, AMIE II may or may not connect, depending on the exact problem. The user should test the AMIE II connection.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/318.png)

<span id="_Ref514864814" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑3. VistA Server Unavailable Alert.

## VistA Limits Ability to See Patient Records

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑4), if the medical facility made local permission modifications in VistA that prohibit users from seeing the contents of patient records.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/319.png)

<span id="_Ref514864786" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑4. User Permission Restricted to View Alert.

## Network Problems

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑5) if the connection to the medical facility is lost unexpectedly. The user should try to connect to the medical facility again.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/320.png)

<span id="_Ref514864741" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑5. Connection to Medical Facility Unavailable Alert.

## Institution File in VistA has Been Locally Modified

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user may receive the message in (Figure 3‑6. Incorrect Regional Office List Alert.) when requesting exams, requesting 7131s, or entering new patients if the VHA medical facility has an incomplete or incorrect Regional Office list in their system.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/321.png)

<span id="_Ref514864712" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑6. Incorrect Regional Office List Alert.

## Too Many Invalid Attempts at Access Code / Verify Code

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user receives the message in (Figure 3‑7. Multiple Invalid Login Attempts Alert.), if the user attempted to log on and entered the wrong Access Code / Verify Code combination three or more times.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/322.png)

<span id="_Ref514864676" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑7. Multiple Invalid Login Attempts Alert.

## Multiple Sign-Ons

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user may receive the message in (Figure 3‑8. Multiple Sign-on Alert.), if the user did not log out of CAPRI correctly or if the session was unexpectedly disconnected.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/323.png)

<span id="_Ref514864654" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑8. Multiple Sign-on Alert.

## General Error Message

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

The user may receive the message in (Figure 3‑9), for several reasons. Upon receiving this message, the user should cancel the current task, close CAPRI, and sign on again. If the user receives this error message again, local IRM staff should be contacted for assistance.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/324.png)

<span id="_Ref514864622" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑9. CAPRI Login Error Message.

A warning message is displayed for GUI version of CAPRI that does not match the current version of the VistA patch.

Sample Warning Message 1: For GUI versions of the CAPRI application that do not match the current version of the VistA patch, a warning message displays that the software does not match what is running on the Vista server.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/325.png)

<span id="_Toc218082702" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑10. Sample Software Version Error Message 1

Pressing OK at the screen above will display the message below:

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/327.png)

<span id="_Toc1316027" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑12. Software Version Error Message 3

## Incompatible DBQ Error Message

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Due to the upgrade of CAPRI to Delphi Rapid Application Development (RAD) Studio 10.2 (also known as XE10) a number of DBQs have been upgraded to be compatible. When a user attempts to open or create a DBQ that is not compatible with CAPRI GUI 220.2.7, the following error message is displayed.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/338.png)

<span id="_Toc218082715" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑23. DBQ Error Message

## Permanent Failure DBQ Transmission Error Message

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When a permanent failure occurs during a DBQ transmission, CAPRI will not allow these DBQs to be retransmitted as there was a failure which cannot be resolved. Users who receive this error will be prompted to contact the Service Desk.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/339.png)

<span id="_Toc218082716" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑24. DBQ Permanent Failure Error

## DBQ Transmission Error Message

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

Users will receive the following error message when the DBQ exam result fails to transmit (Figure 3‑25). The transmission will automatically attempt to complete a successful transmission (Figure 3‑26). After five failed attempts, users will receive a failed transmission error message (Figure 3‑27. DBQ Transmission Failure Error). To retransmit, reference [2.13.7](#vba-efolder-queue-button) VBA eFolder Queue Button.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/340.png)

<span id="_Ref94013355" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑25. DBQ Transmission Error

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/341.png)

<span id="_Ref94013456" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑26. DBQ Retransmission Attempt Message

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/342.png)

<span id="_Ref89947566" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑27. DBQ Transmission Failure Error

## NEW PERSON file missing SSN – at Local Site

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When launching CAPRI, if the user is missing their SSN at local site, they will be prompted with the following error message (Figure 3‑28) informing them to submit a ServiceNow ticket or to contact support. After selecting OK, CAPRI will shut down and prevent users from working in CAPRI until the issue is remediated.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/343.png)

<span id="_Ref147910119" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑28 CAPRI has detected user's SSN is missing at local New Person file.

## NEW PERSON file missing SSN – at Remote Local Site

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

When attempting to connect to a remote site, if the site's new person file is missing the user's SSN, the user will receive the following message informing the user to submit a ServiceNow ticket or to contact support to remediate the issue.

Selecting OK on the message (Figure 3‑29) will display the CAPRI Remote site list and allow the user to select another site to continue work on a different patient. If the user selects Cancel (Figure 3‑30) CAPRI will shut down.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/344.png)

<span id="_Ref146620947" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑29 CAPRI has detected NEW PERSON file at local site is missing user's SSN.

![](capri-gui-user-manual-updated-dvba-2-7-255/345.png)

<span id="_Ref146620964" class="anchor"></span>Figure 3‑30 Select New Site

# CAPRI Equivalents for AMIE II Functions

<!-- back-to-toc -->[↑ Table of Contents](#table-of-contents)

<span id="_Toc218082368" class="anchor"></span>Table 4‑1. CAPRI Equivalents for AMIE II Functions

| AMIE Function                                           | CAPRI Function                                                                             |
|---------------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Admission Inquiry by Date (All Admissions)              | Admission Inquiry By Date                                                                  |
| Print New Notices of Discharge                          | Print New Notices of Discharge                                                             |
| Add an Exam to an Existing Request                      | C&P Exams – View/Edit Selected Request – Add Exam to Request                               |
| Ad Hoc Health Summary                                   | Health Summaries – Adhoc Report                                                            |
| Admission Report for Service Connected Veterans         | CAPRI Reports – Admission Report for SC Veterans                                           |
| AMIS 290 for the Regional Office                        | CAPRI Reports – AMIS 290                                                                   |
| Beneficiary Information Status Inquiry                  | 7131 Request – Status Inquiry                                                              |
| Cancel C&P Requests (all exams)                         | C&P Exams – View/Edit Selected Request – Cancel ALL Exams                                  |
| Cancel C&P Requests (selected exams)                    | C&P Exams – View/Edit Selected Request – Select Exam – View Exam – Cancel This Exam        |
| Detailed Inpatient Inquiry                              | Reports – Detailed Inpt. Inquiry                                                           |
| Discharge Report (A&A, Pension, Service Connected, All) | CAPRI Reports – Discharge Report (A&A, Pension, SC, All)                                   |
| Edit 7131 Remarks                                       | 7131 Request – View/Edit Selected Request                                                  |
| Edit C&P Request Information                            | C&P Exams – View/Edit Selected Request                                                     |
| Edit Patient Address Information                        | Admin – Edit Address Information                                                           |
| Enter a C&P Exam Request                                | C&P Exams – Add a New Request                                                              |
| Full Patient Profile MAS                                | Reports – Patient Profile MAS (Full)                                                       |
| HS Health Summary Menu                                  | Health Summaries tab                                                                       |
| Incompetent Veterans Report                             | CAPRI Reports – Incompetent Veterans Report                                                |

# Glossary

<span id="_Toc218082384" class="anchor"></span>Table 6‑1. Glossary
| Term          | Definition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| A&A               | Aid and Attendance                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| AMIE              | Automated Medical Information Exchange                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| AMIS              | Automated Management Information System                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ASU               | Authorization/Subscription Utility                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| BDD               | Benefits Delivery at Discharge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| CAC               | Clinical Applications Coordinator. The CAC is a person at a hospital or clinic assigned to coordinate the installation, maintenance and upgrading of CPRS and other VistA software programs for the end users.                                                                                                 |
| CAP               | Combined Assessment Program                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| CAPRI             | Compensation and Pension Record Interchange                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| CCR               | CAPRI Contract Referral                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| CNH               | Community Nursing Home                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Consults          | Consult/Request Tracking, a VistA product that is also part of CPRS (it can function as part of CPRS, independently as a standalone package, or as part of TIU). It is used to request and track consultations or procedures from one clinician to another clinician or service.                               |
| C-Number          | Claim Number                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| C&P               | Compensation and Pension                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| CPEP              | Compensation and Pension Examination Program                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| CPRS              | Computerized Patient Record System, the VistA package (in both GUI and character-based formats) that provides access to most components of the patient chart                                                                                                                                                   |
| CPWM              | Compensation and Pension Worksheet Module                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| DBQ               | Disability Benefits Questionnaire                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| DES               | Disability Evaluation System                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Discharge Summary | Component of TIU that can function as part of CPRS, Discharge Summaries are recapitulations of a patient's course of care while in the hospital.                                                                                                                                                               |
| DES               | Disability Evaluation System                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DOM               | Domiciliary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| DROs              | Data Request Outputs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DVBA              | On Front Cover                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| FBCNH             | Fee Basis Community Nursing Home                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| FHIE              | Federal Health Information Exchange                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| GUI               | Graphical User Interface – Windows-like screen with pull-down menus, icons, pointer device, etc. on front cover                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Health Summary    | VistA product that can be viewed through CPRS, Health Summaries are components of patient information extracted from other VistA applications                                                                                                                                                                  |
| HDR               | Health Data Repository                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| HIA               | Health Information Access                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Imaging           | VistA product that is also a component of CPRS; includes Radiology, X-rays, Nuclear Medicine, etc.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| IRM               | Information Resources Management                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| MAS               | Medical Administration Service                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| MVI               | Master Veteran Index                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| OI                | Office of Information                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| OIG               | Office of the Inspector General                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| O/P               | Out Patient                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| PDF               | Portable Document Format                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| RDV               | Remote Data View                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| RO                | Regional Office                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| RPC               | Remote Procedure Call                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| RSVRs             | Ratings Veterans Services Representatives                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| SSN               | Social Security Number                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

TIU components can be accessed for individual patients through the CPRS, or for multiple patients through the TIU interface. |
